# Step 25 — Certificate-family manifest

**Certificate-family manifest** — реестр всех finite blocks: параметры, CSV-файлы, хэши, safe lower bounds, статус `PASS/FAIL`, и связь с theorem-contract.

**Finite block** — один конкретный сертифицированный уровень, например:

\[
k=11,\quad L=3.0,\quad \ell=0.30,\quad \Delta=0.25,\quad \kappa=3.25,\quad \theta=10^{-4}.
\]

**Manifest** — таблица, которая превращает разрозненные certificate files в управляемое семейство.

---

## 1. Что должен делать Step 25

Не считать матрицы заново вручную.  
Не запускать sweep вслепую.

Он должен:

1. брать список finite blocks;
2. проверять, что midpoint/radius CSV существуют;
3. считать `sha256` для каждого CSV;
4. запускать Step 18 guard в `--mode radius`;
5. вытаскивать:
   \[
   D_\theta\text{ safe lower},
   \qquad
   R_\kappa\text{ safe lower};
   \]
6. писать единый manifest CSV;
7. писать JSON summary;
8. помечать block как:
   - `PASS`;
   - `FAIL`;
   - `MISSING_FILES`;
   - `RUN_ERROR`.

---

# 2. Script

Создай:

```bash
q3.lean.aristotle/scripts/q3_psdpd_step25_family_manifest.py
```

Код:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Step 25 PSD-pd certificate-family manifest.

Purpose:
  Build a registry of interval-backed finite certificate blocks.

For each block:
  - verify midpoint/radius CSV paths
  - compute SHA256 hashes
  - run Step 18 penalty guard in --mode radius
  - parse Dtheta/Rkappa safe lower bounds
  - write manifest CSV + JSON summary

This does NOT create new certificates.
It organizes already-created finite proof blocks into a family/ledger.

Notation:
  k_spline = B-spline degree
  ell      = bump scale
  delta    = grid spacing
  kappa    = kappa-split parameter
  theta    = strengthened certificate parameter
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import re
import subprocess
import sys
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional


# ----------------------------
# Data model
# ----------------------------

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BlockConfig:
    block_id: str
    family_id: str
    L: float
    k_spline: int
    ell: float
    delta: float
    kappa: float
    theta: float
    midpoint_csv: str
    radius_csv: str
    tau_grid: str = "log:-8:8:161"
    role: str = "candidate"
    notes: str = ""


@dataclass
class BlockResult:
    block_id: str
    family_id: str
    role: str

    L: float
    k_spline: int
    ell: float
    delta: float
    kappa: float
    theta: float
    tau_grid: str

    midpoint_csv: str
    radius_csv: str
    midpoint_sha256: str
    radius_sha256: str

    dtheta_safe_lower: Optional[float]
    rkappa_safe_lower: Optional[float]
    dtheta_pass: Optional[bool]
    rkappa_pass: Optional[bool]
    status: str

    stdout_path: str
    notes: str


# ----------------------------
# Helpers
# ----------------------------

def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()


def parse_float_maybe(text: str) -> Optional[float]:
    try:
        return float(text)
    except Exception:
        return None


def parse_step18_output(stdout: str) -> tuple[Optional[float], Optional[float], Optional[bool], Optional[bool]]:
    """
    Parse Step 18 output.

    Expected sections roughly:

      == Dtheta = C - theta R_kappa penalty certificate ==
      ...
      safe_lower = ...

      == R_kappa penalty certificate ==
      ...
      safe_lower = ...

    Returns:
      dtheta_safe_lower, rkappa_safe_lower, dtheta_pass, rkappa_pass
    """
    dtheta_safe: Optional[float] = None
    rkappa_safe: Optional[float] = None
    dtheta_pass: Optional[bool] = None
    rkappa_pass: Optional[bool] = None

    current = None

    safe_re = re.compile(r"safe_lower\s*=\s*([-+0-9.eE]+)")
    pass_re = re.compile(r"PASS\s*=\s*(True|False)")

    for raw_line in stdout.splitlines():
        line = raw_line.strip()

        if line.startswith("==") and "Dtheta" in line:
            current = "Dtheta"
        elif line.startswith("==") and "R_kappa" in line:
            current = "Rkappa"
        elif line.startswith("==") and "Rkappa" in line:
            current = "Rkappa"

        m_safe = safe_re.search(line)
        if m_safe and current:
            val = parse_float_maybe(m_safe.group(1))
            if current == "Dtheta":
                dtheta_safe = val
            elif current == "Rkappa":
                rkappa_safe = val

        m_pass = pass_re.search(line)
        if m_pass and current:
            val = m_pass.group(1) == "True"
            if current == "Dtheta":
                dtheta_pass = val
            elif current == "Rkappa":
                rkappa_pass = val

    if dtheta_pass is None and dtheta_safe is not None:
        dtheta_pass = dtheta_safe > 0.0
    if rkappa_pass is None and rkappa_safe is not None:
        rkappa_pass = rkappa_safe > 0.0

    return dtheta_safe, rkappa_safe, dtheta_pass, rkappa_pass


def read_blocks_csv(path: Path) -> list[BlockConfig]:
    blocks: list[BlockConfig] = []

    with path.open() as f:
        reader = csv.DictReader(f)
        for row in reader:
            blocks.append(
                BlockConfig(
                    block_id=row["block_id"],
                    family_id=row.get("family_id", "psdpd_family_v1"),
                    L=float(row["L"]),
                    k_spline=int(row["k_spline"]),
                    ell=float(row["ell"]),
                    delta=float(row["delta"]),
                    kappa=float(row["kappa"]),
                    theta=float(row["theta"]),
                    midpoint_csv=row["midpoint_csv"],
                    radius_csv=row["radius_csv"],
                    tau_grid=row.get("tau_grid", "log:-8:8:161"),
                    role=row.get("role", "candidate"),
                    notes=row.get("notes", ""),
                )
            )

    return blocks


def write_default_blocks_csv(path: Path) -> None:
    """
    Writes a seed block list for the current primary/control candidates.

    Adjust file names if your actual Step 22 CSV names differ.
    """
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    rows = [
        {
            "block_id": "psdpd_L3_k11_ell030_delta025_theta1e4",
            "family_id": "psdpd_family_v1",
            "L": "3.0",
            "k_spline": "11",
            "ell": "0.30",
            "delta": "0.25",
            "kappa": "3.25",
            "theta": "1e-4",
            "midpoint_csv": "q3.lean.aristotle/docs/insights/q3_psdpd_step22_midpoints_k11.csv",
            "radius_csv": "q3.lean.aristotle/docs/insights/q3_psdpd_step22_radii_k11.csv",
            "tau_grid": "log:-8:8:161",
            "role": "primary",
            "notes": "Primary interval-backed finite block after Step 22.",
        },
        {
            "block_id": "psdpd_L3_k9_ell030_delta025_theta1e5",
            "family_id": "psdpd_family_v1",
            "L": "3.0",
            "k_spline": "9",
            "ell": "0.30",
            "delta": "0.25",
            "kappa": "3.075",
            "theta": "1e-5",
            "midpoint_csv": "q3.lean.aristotle/docs/insights/q3_psdpd_step22_midpoints_k9.csv",
            "radius_csv": "q3.lean.aristotle/docs/insights/q3_psdpd_step22_radii_k9.csv",
            "tau_grid": "log:-8:8:161",
            "role": "control",
            "notes": "Control interval-backed finite block after Step 22.",
        },
    ]

    with path.open("w", newline="") as f:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(
            f,
            fieldnames=[
                "block_id",
                "family_id",
                "L",
                "k_spline",
                "ell",
                "delta",
                "kappa",
                "theta",
                "midpoint_csv",
                "radius_csv",
                "tau_grid",
                "role",
                "notes",
            ],
        )
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)


def run_step18(
    repo_root: Path,
    step18_script: Path,
    block: BlockConfig,
    stdout_dir: Path,
) -> BlockResult:
    mid_path = repo_root / block.midpoint_csv
    rad_path = repo_root / block.radius_csv

    stdout_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stdout_path = stdout_dir / f"{block.block_id}.step18.txt"

    if not mid_path.exists() or not rad_path.exists():
        missing = []
        if not mid_path.exists():
            missing.append(str(mid_path))
        if not rad_path.exists():
            missing.append(str(rad_path))

        stdout_path.write_text("Missing files:\n" + "\n".join(missing) + "\n")

        return BlockResult(
            block_id=block.block_id,
            family_id=block.family_id,
            role=block.role,
            L=block.L,
            k_spline=block.k_spline,
            ell=block.ell,
            delta=block.delta,
            kappa=block.kappa,
            theta=block.theta,
            tau_grid=block.tau_grid,
            midpoint_csv=block.midpoint_csv,
            radius_csv=block.radius_csv,
            midpoint_sha256="MISSING",
            radius_sha256="MISSING",
            dtheta_safe_lower=None,
            rkappa_safe_lower=None,
            dtheta_pass=None,
            rkappa_pass=None,
            status="MISSING_FILES",
            stdout_path=str(stdout_path.relative_to(repo_root)),
            notes=block.notes,
        )

    cmd = [
        sys.executable,
        str(step18_script),
        "--L",
        str(block.L),
        "--k-spline",
        str(block.k_spline),
        "--ell",
        str(block.ell),
        "--delta",
        str(block.delta),
        "--kappa",
        str(block.kappa),
        "--theta",
        str(block.theta),
        "--mode",
        "radius",
        "--midpoint-csv",
        block.midpoint_csv,
        "--radius-csv",
        block.radius_csv,
        "--tau-grid",
        block.tau_grid,
    ]

    proc = subprocess.run(
        cmd,
        cwd=repo_root,
        text=True,
        stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
        stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
        check=False,
    )

    stdout_path.write_text(proc.stdout)

    dtheta_safe, rkappa_safe, dtheta_pass, rkappa_pass = parse_step18_output(proc.stdout)

    if proc.returncode != 0:
        status = "RUN_ERROR"
    elif dtheta_pass and rkappa_pass:
        status = "PASS"
    else:
        status = "FAIL"

    return BlockResult(
        block_id=block.block_id,
        family_id=block.family_id,
        role=block.role,
        L=block.L,
        k_spline=block.k_spline,
        ell=block.ell,
        delta=block.delta,
        kappa=block.kappa,
        theta=block.theta,
        tau_grid=block.tau_grid,
        midpoint_csv=block.midpoint_csv,
        radius_csv=block.radius_csv,
        midpoint_sha256=sha256_file(mid_path),
        radius_sha256=sha256_file(rad_path),
        dtheta_safe_lower=dtheta_safe,
        rkappa_safe_lower=rkappa_safe,
        dtheta_pass=dtheta_pass,
        rkappa_pass=rkappa_pass,
        status=status,
        stdout_path=str(stdout_path.relative_to(repo_root)),
        notes=block.notes,
    )


def write_manifest_csv(path: Path, results: list[BlockResult]) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    with path.open("w", newline="") as f:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=list(asdict(results[0]).keys()))
        writer.writeheader()
        for r in results:
            writer.writerow(asdict(r))


def write_summary_json(path: Path, results: list[BlockResult]) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    summary = {
        "total_blocks": len(results),
        "pass_blocks": sum(1 for r in results if r.status == "PASS"),
        "fail_blocks": sum(1 for r in results if r.status == "FAIL"),
        "missing_blocks": sum(1 for r in results if r.status == "MISSING_FILES"),
        "run_error_blocks": sum(1 for r in results if r.status == "RUN_ERROR"),
        "primary_pass": [
            r.block_id for r in results if r.role == "primary" and r.status == "PASS"
        ],
        "control_pass": [
            r.block_id for r in results if r.role == "control" and r.status == "PASS"
        ],
        "blocks": [asdict(r) for r in results],
    }

    path.write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2, sort_keys=True))


def print_results(results: list[BlockResult]) -> None:
    print("\nCertificate-family manifest")
    print(
        "status        role       block_id                                      "
        "Dtheta_safe        Rkappa_safe"
    )

    for r in results:
        d = "NA" if r.dtheta_safe_lower is None else f"{r.dtheta_safe_lower:.6e}"
        k = "NA" if r.rkappa_safe_lower is None else f"{r.rkappa_safe_lower:.6e}"
        print(f"{r.status:<13} {r.role:<10} {r.block_id:<45} {d:>14} {k:>14}")


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()

    parser.add_argument(
        "--blocks-csv",
        default="q3.lean.aristotle/docs/insights/q3_psdpd_family_blocks_seed.csv",
        help="Input CSV describing finite certificate blocks.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--write-default-blocks",
        action="store_true",
        help="Write a default blocks CSV for current k=11 primary and k=9 control.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--out-manifest",
        default="q3.lean.aristotle/docs/insights/q3_psdpd_certificate_family_manifest.csv",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--out-summary",
        default="q3.lean.aristotle/docs/insights/q3_psdpd_certificate_family_manifest.json",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--stdout-dir",
        default="q3.lean.aristotle/docs/insights/q3_psdpd_family_step18_outputs",
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    repo_root = Path.cwd()
    blocks_csv = repo_root / args.blocks_csv

    if args.write_default_blocks:
        write_default_blocks_csv(blocks_csv)
        print(f"Wrote default blocks CSV: {blocks_csv}")

    if not blocks_csv.exists():
        raise SystemExit(
            f"Blocks CSV does not exist: {blocks_csv}\n"
            "Run with --write-default-blocks first or provide --blocks-csv."
        )

    step18_script = repo_root / "q3.lean.aristotle/scripts/q3_psdpd_step18_interval_guard.py"
    if not step18_script.exists():
        raise SystemExit(f"Step 18 script not found: {step18_script}")

    blocks = read_blocks_csv(blocks_csv)

    print("== Step 25 certificate-family manifest ==")
    print(f"repo_root={repo_root}")
    print(f"blocks_csv={blocks_csv}")
    print(f"blocks={len(blocks)}")

    results = [
        run_step18(
            repo_root=repo_root,
            step18_script=step18_script,
            block=block,
            stdout_dir=repo_root / args.stdout_dir,
        )
        for block in blocks
    ]

    out_manifest = repo_root / args.out_manifest
    out_summary = repo_root / args.out_summary

    write_manifest_csv(out_manifest, results)
    write_summary_json(out_summary, results)

    print_results(results)
    print(f"\nWrote manifest: {out_manifest}")
    print(f"Wrote summary:  {out_summary}")

    if any(r.status != "PASS" for r in results):
        raise SystemExit("Some blocks did not PASS.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

- **`--write-default-blocks`** — создать стартовый список: primary `k=11` и control `k=9`.
- **`--out-manifest`** — итоговый manifest CSV.
- **`--out-summary`** — JSON summary.
- **`--stdout-dir`** — папка, куда сохраняется полный stdout каждого Step 18 запуска.

# 5. Что считать PASS

В manifest должны быть строки:

```text
PASS primary ...
PASS control ...
```

И в CSV:

```csv
status,dtheta_safe_lower,rkappa_safe_lower
PASS,1.2228e-04,1.3569e-01
PASS,1.2636e-05,1.9590e-03
```

Цифры могут чуть отличаться из-за текущего Step 18 output, но знак должен быть:

\[
\boxed{
D_\theta\text{ safe lower}>0
}
\]

\[
\boxed{
R_\kappa\text{ safe lower}>0.
}
\]

```md
# Step 25 — Certificate-family manifest

## Goal

Turn interval-backed finite blocks into a managed certificate family.

This does not create new certificates. It records:

- block parameters;
- midpoint/radius CSV paths;
- SHA256 hashes;
- Step 18 penalty-guard safe lower bounds;
- PASS/FAIL status.

## Current family

| role | block_id | k | ell | delta | kappa | theta | status | Dtheta safe | Rkappa safe |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|---:|---:|

## Files

- seed blocks:
  `docs/insights/q3_psdpd_family_blocks_seed.csv`
- manifest:
  `docs/insights/q3_psdpd_certificate_family_manifest.csv`
- summary:
  `docs/insights/q3_psdpd_certificate_family_manifest.json`
- Step 18 outputs:
  `docs/insights/q3_psdpd_family_step18_outputs/`

## Theorem meaning

Each PASS block proves a finite interval-backed penalty certificate:

\[
D_\theta+\tau Q^TQ\succ0,
\qquad
R_\kappa+\tau Q^TQ\succ0.
\]

Therefore, on the corresponding finite boundary-null space:

\[
C^\circ\succeq \theta R_\kappa^\circ\succ0.
\]

## Why this matters

Step 22 gave one proof-grade finite block.

Step 25 starts the transition from a single finite block to a certificate family,
which is the engineering side of the Step 23 exhaustion contract.

## Verdict

...
```

## ASSISTANT
Ы. Да, вот теперь следующий шаг точный: **Step 26 — `FiniteCert` ledger consumer**.

Мы больше не “верим CSV”. Мы превращаем строки `PASS` из manifest в **Lean-facing finite predicates**, чтобы **Step 23** мог ссылаться не на файлы и stdout, а на объект вида:

\[
\boxed{
\texttt{HasFinitePenaltyCert(block)}
}
\]

Это не доказывает exhaustion. Это делает правильный интерфейс:

\[
\boxed{
\text{interval-backed block}
\Rightarrow
\text{finite certificate predicate}
\Rightarrow
\text{Step 23 family/exhaustion consumer}
}
\]

**FiniteCert** — формальный объект, описывающий один конечный сертифицированный block.  
**Ledger** — список таких объектов с параметрами, хэшами и нижними safe bounds.  
**Predicate** — утверждение в Lean-стиле, например `HasFinitePenaltyCert c`, которое downstream-теоремы могут принимать как гипотезу.

   - `family_id`;
   - `role`;
   - параметры `L`, `k_spline`, `ell`, `delta`, `kappa`, `theta`;
   - `sha256`;
   - conservative rational floors для `Dtheta` и `Rkappa`.

**Conservative floor** — безопасно заниженная рациональная нижняя граница. Например, если safe lower `1.222859e-4`, можно положить floor `1e-4`.

---

# 2. Script

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Step 26 PSD-pd FiniteCert ledger generator.

Reads the Step 25 certificate-family manifest and emits:

  1. Lean ledger:
     Q3/Proofs/PSDpd/FiniteCertLedger.lean

  2. JSON ledger:
     docs/insights/q3_psdpd_finitecert_ledger.json

The Lean file does NOT re-prove interval matrices.
It creates formal finite predicates from already audited PASS manifest rows.

Notation:
  FiniteCertBlock = one interval-backed finite block.
  HasFinitePenaltyCert = Lean-facing predicate for Step 23 consumers.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
import math
import re
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from decimal import Decimal, ROUND_FLOOR, getcontext
from pathlib import Path


getcontext().prec = 80


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LedgerRow:
    block_id: str
    family_id: str
    role: str

    L: str
    k_spline: str
    ell: str
    delta: str
    kappa: str
    theta: str

    midpoint_csv: str
    radius_csv: str
    midpoint_sha256: str
    radius_sha256: str

    dtheta_safe_lower: str
    rkappa_safe_lower: str

    dtheta_floor_num: int
    dtheta_floor_den: int
    rkappa_floor_num: int
    rkappa_floor_den: int


def sanitize_ident(text: str) -> str:
    """
    Convert block_id to a Lean-safe identifier suffix.
    """
    s = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_]", "_", text)
    s = re.sub(r"_+", "_", s).strip("_")
    if not s:
        s = "block"
    if s[0].isdigit():
        s = "block_" + s
    return s


def lean_string(text: str) -> str:
    return text.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace('"', '\\"')


def decimal_floor_rat(value: str, digits: int) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """
    Convert positive decimal string to conservative floor rational.

    Example:
      value=0.0001222859, digits=8
      den=1e8
      num=floor(value*den)
    """
    d = Decimal(str(value))
    if d <= 0:
        return 0, 1

    den = 10**digits
    scaled = (d * Decimal(den)).to_integral_value(rounding=ROUND_FLOOR)
    num = int(scaled)

    # Ensure nonzero if value is positive but digits too low.
    if num <= 0:
        # Increase denominator adaptively.
        for extra in range(digits + 1, digits + 80):
            den2 = 10**extra
            scaled2 = (d * Decimal(den2)).to_integral_value(rounding=ROUND_FLOOR)
            num2 = int(scaled2)
            if num2 > 0:
                return num2, den2
        raise ValueError(f"Could not produce positive floor for {value}")

    g = math.gcd(num, den)
    return num // g, den // g


def read_manifest(path: Path, floor_digits: int) -> list[LedgerRow]:
    rows: list[LedgerRow] = []

    with path.open() as f:
        reader = csv.DictReader(f)

        for row in reader:
            status = row.get("status", "").strip().lower()
            if status != "pass":
                continue

            dtheta = row["dtheta_safe_lower"]
            rkappa = row["rkappa_safe_lower"]

            d_num, d_den = decimal_floor_rat(dtheta, floor_digits)
            r_num, r_den = decimal_floor_rat(rkappa, floor_digits)

            rows.append(
                LedgerRow(
                    block_id=row["block_id"],
                    family_id=row.get("family_id", "psdpd_family_v1"),
                    role=row.get("role", "candidate"),

                    L=row["L"],
                    k_spline=row["k_spline"],
                    ell=row["ell"],
                    delta=row["delta"],
                    kappa=row["kappa"],
                    theta=row["theta"],

                    midpoint_csv=row["midpoint_csv"],
                    radius_csv=row["radius_csv"],
                    midpoint_sha256=row.get("midpoint_sha256", ""),
                    radius_sha256=row.get("radius_sha256", ""),

                    dtheta_safe_lower=dtheta,
                    rkappa_safe_lower=rkappa,

                    dtheta_floor_num=d_num,
                    dtheta_floor_den=d_den,
                    rkappa_floor_num=r_num,
                    rkappa_floor_den=r_den,
                )
            )

    return rows


def emit_lean(rows: list[LedgerRow], out: Path) -> None:
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    lines: list[str] = []

    lines.append("import Mathlib.Data.Rat.Basic")
    lines.append("import Mathlib.Tactic")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("namespace Q3")
    lines.append("namespace PSDpd")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("/--")
    lines.append("A finite interval-backed PSD-pd certificate block.")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("This ledger object records audited manifest data.")
    lines.append("It does not recompute the interval matrices inside Lean.")
    lines.append("-/")
    lines.append("structure FiniteCertBlock where")
    lines.append("  blockId : String")
    lines.append("  familyId : String")
    lines.append("  role : String")
    lines.append("  L : String")
    lines.append("  kSpline : String")
    lines.append("  ell : String")
    lines.append("  delta : String")
    lines.append("  kappa : String")
    lines.append("  theta : String")
    lines.append("  midpointCsv : String")
    lines.append("  radiusCsv : String")
    lines.append("  midpointSha256 : String")
    lines.append("  radiusSha256 : String")
    lines.append("  dthetaSafeLowerDecimal : String")
    lines.append("  rkappaSafeLowerDecimal : String")
    lines.append("  dthetaSafeFloor : Rat")
    lines.append("  rkappaSafeFloor : Rat")
    lines.append("  dthetaSafeFloor_pos : 0 < dthetaSafeFloor")
    lines.append("  rkappaSafeFloor_pos : 0 < rkappaSafeFloor")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("/-- Lean-facing predicate consumed by Step 23 finite-family theorems. -/")
    lines.append("structure HasFinitePenaltyCert (c : FiniteCertBlock) : Prop where")
    lines.append("  dtheta_pos : 0 < c.dthetaSafeFloor")
    lines.append("  rkappa_pos : 0 < c.rkappaSafeFloor")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("structure FiniteCertLedger where")
    lines.append("  certs : List FiniteCertBlock")
    lines.append("")

    cert_names = []

    for row in rows:
        name = sanitize_ident(row.block_id)
        cert_names.append(name)

        lines.append(f"def {name} : FiniteCertBlock :=")
        lines.append("{")
        lines.append(f'  blockId := "{lean_string(row.block_id)}",')
        lines.append(f'  familyId := "{lean_string(row.family_id)}",')
        lines.append(f'  role := "{lean_string(row.role)}",')
        lines.append(f'  L := "{lean_string(row.L)}",')
        lines.append(f'  kSpline := "{lean_string(row.k_spline)}",')
        lines.append(f'  ell := "{lean_string(row.ell)}",')
        lines.append(f'  delta := "{lean_string(row.delta)}",')
        lines.append(f'  kappa := "{lean_string(row.kappa)}",')
        lines.append(f'  theta := "{lean_string(row.theta)}",')
        lines.append(f'  midpointCsv := "{lean_string(row.midpoint_csv)}",')
        lines.append(f'  radiusCsv := "{lean_string(row.radius_csv)}",')
        lines.append(f'  midpointSha256 := "{lean_string(row.midpoint_sha256)}",')
        lines.append(f'  radiusSha256 := "{lean_string(row.radius_sha256)}",')
        lines.append(f'  dthetaSafeLowerDecimal := "{lean_string(row.dtheta_safe_lower)}",')
        lines.append(f'  rkappaSafeLowerDecimal := "{lean_string(row.rkappa_safe_lower)}",')
        lines.append(
            f"  dthetaSafeFloor := ({row.dtheta_floor_num} : Rat) / {row.dtheta_floor_den},"
        )
        lines.append(
            f"  rkappaSafeFloor := ({row.rkappa_floor_num} : Rat) / {row.rkappa_floor_den},"
        )
        lines.append("  dthetaSafeFloor_pos := by norm_num,")
        lines.append("  rkappaSafeFloor_pos := by norm_num")
        lines.append("}")
        lines.append("")
        lines.append(f"theorem {name}_hasFinitePenaltyCert : HasFinitePenaltyCert {name} :=")
        lines.append("{")
        lines.append(f"  dtheta_pos := {name}.dthetaSafeFloor_pos,")
        lines.append(f"  rkappa_pos := {name}.rkappaSafeFloor_pos")
        lines.append("}")
        lines.append("")

    list_expr = "[" + ", ".join(cert_names) + "]"

    lines.append("def currentFiniteCertLedger : FiniteCertLedger :=")
    lines.append("{")
    lines.append(f"  certs := {list_expr}")
    lines.append("}")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("end PSDpd")
    lines.append("end Q3")
    lines.append("")

    out.write_text("\n".join(lines))


def emit_json(rows: list[LedgerRow], out: Path) -> None:
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    payload = {
        "schema": "q3_psdpd_finitecert_ledger_v1",
        "pass_blocks": len(rows),
        "blocks": [asdict(row) for row in rows],
    }

    out.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, sort_keys=True))


def run() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()

    parser.add_argument(
        "--manifest",
        default="q3.lean.aristotle/docs/insights/q3_psdpd_certificate_family_manifest.csv",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--out-lean",
        default="q3.lean.aristotle/Q3/Proofs/PSDpd/FiniteCertLedger.lean",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--out-json",
        default="q3.lean.aristotle/docs/insights/q3_psdpd_finitecert_ledger.json",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--floor-digits",
        type=int,
        default=12,
        help="Decimal digits for conservative rational safe lower floors.",
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    manifest = Path(args.manifest)
    if not manifest.exists():
        raise SystemExit(f"Manifest not found: {manifest}")

    rows = read_manifest(manifest, floor_digits=args.floor_digits)

    if not rows:
        raise SystemExit("No PASS rows found in manifest.")

    emit_lean(rows, Path(args.out_lean))
    emit_json(rows, Path(args.out_json))

    print("== Step 26 FiniteCert ledger ==")
    print(f"PASS rows: {len(rows)}")
    print(f"Wrote Lean: {args.out_lean}")
    print(f"Wrote JSON: {args.out_json}")

    for row in rows:
        print(
            f"- {row.block_id}: "
            f"Dtheta floor={row.dtheta_floor_num}/{row.dtheta_floor_den}, "
            f"Rkappa floor={row.rkappa_floor_num}/{row.rkappa_floor_den}"
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run()
```

# 3. Run

```bash
uv run python q3.lean.aristotle/scripts/q3_psdpd_step26_finitecert_ledger.py \
  --manifest q3.lean.aristotle/docs/insights/q3_psdpd_certificate_family_manifest.csv \
  --out-lean q3.lean.aristotle/Q3/Proofs/PSDpd/FiniteCertLedger.lean \
  --out-json q3.lean.aristotle/docs/insights/q3_psdpd_finitecert_ledger.json \
  --floor-digits 12
```

Потом Lean check:

```bash
cd q3.lean.aristotle
lake env lean Q3/Proofs/PSDpd/FiniteCertLedger.lean
```

**Подстановка переменных**:

- **`--manifest`** — manifest CSV из Step 25.
- **`--out-lean`** — Lean ledger file.
- **`--out-json`** — audit JSON.
- **`--floor-digits 12`** — насколько точно занижать safe lower bounds. Например, `12` значит denominator примерно \(10^{12}\), но значение всё равно floor, не rounding.

# 4. Что это даст в Lean

Lean-файл будет содержать примерно:

```lean
def psdpd_L3_k11_ell030_delta025_theta1e4 : FiniteCertBlock := ...

theorem psdpd_L3_k11_ell030_delta025_theta1e4_hasFinitePenaltyCert :
    HasFinitePenaltyCert psdpd_L3_k11_ell030_delta025_theta1e4 := ...
```

То есть Step 23 сможет принимать:

```lean
(hcert : HasFinitePenaltyCert block)
```

и уже не ссылаться напрямую на CSV/stdout.

---

# 5. Что важно не переобещать

Этот ledger **не доказывает внутри Lean**, что матрицы действительно SPD.

Он формализует уже проверенный **audit artifact**:

\[
\boxed{
\text{manifest PASS row}
\Rightarrow
\text{Lean-facing certificate predicate}
}
\]

Полный proof-grade путь дальше:

\[
\boxed{
\text{Step 26 ledger}
\Rightarrow
\text{Step 27 finite-family theorem shell}
\Rightarrow
\text{Step 28 exhaustion consumer}
}
\]

То есть это мост, не финальная теорема.

# Step 27 — Directed family skeleton

Сейчас у нас есть:

\[
\boxed{
\texttt{PASS manifest row}
\Rightarrow
\texttt{FinitePenaltyCert}
\Rightarrow
\text{finite boundary-null positivity}
}
\]

Следующий ход:

\[
\boxed{
\texttt{FinitePenaltyCert}
\Rightarrow
\texttt{DirectedFiniteCertFamily}
\Rightarrow
\texttt{Step 23 exhaustion consumer}
}
\]

**Directed family** — семейство finite blocks, упорядоченное по “refinement”: большее окно, меньший `ell`, богаче basis, сильнее покрытие.  
**Exhaustion** — доказательство, что объединение finite spaces плотно покрывает нужный бесконечный класс тестов.  
**Boundary-null exhaustion** — то же самое, но с сохранением условий \(H(1/2)=H(-1/2)=0\).  
**Ledger consumer** — Lean/API слой, который читает уже принятые `FinitePenaltyCert` и подаёт их в theorem-shell.

---

# Что делаем в Step 27

Не доказываем exhaustion сразу. Сначала создаём формальный интерфейс:

\[
\boxed{
\text{“если есть directed certified family, и она exhausting, то получаем boundary-null positivity.”}
}
\]

То есть Step 27 — это **Lean skeleton**, не новый numerical pipeline.

---

## 1. Lean-файл

Создать:

```bash
q3.lean.aristotle/Q3/Proofs/PSD_CertificateFamily.lean
```

Содержимое-скелет:

```lean
import Q3.Proofs.PSD_PenaltyCertificate

namespace Q3
namespace PSDpd

/--
A finite test-space label.

This is intentionally abstract: the analytic realization of the space
is handled later by the exhaustion layer.
-/
structure FiniteSpaceLabel where
  id : String
  L : String
  ell : String
  delta : String
  kSpline : String
deriving Repr

/--
A finite certified block is a finite space together with a penalty certificate.
-/
structure CertifiedFiniteBlock where
  label : FiniteSpaceLabel
  cert : FinitePenaltyCert

/--
A directed relation between finite blocks.

`Refines A B` means: block `B` is at least as rich as block `A`.
Examples later:
- larger support window;
- smaller bump scale;
- denser grid;
- higher local polynomial degree;
- stronger certificate family coverage.
-/
class HasRefinement (α : Type) where
  Refines : α → α → Prop

/--
Abstract directed family of finite certified blocks.

This does not yet assert analytic density. It only records the order-theoretic
shape needed by the exhaustion theorem.
-/
structure DirectedCertFamily where
  Block : Type
  refinement : HasRefinement Block
  certBlock : Block → CertifiedFiniteBlock

  /-- Nonempty family. -/
  nonempty : ∃ b : Block, True

  /-- Directedness: any two blocks admit a common refinement. -/
  directed :
    ∀ b₁ b₂ : Block,
      ∃ b₃ : Block,
        refinement.Refines b₁ b₃ ∧ refinement.Refines b₂ b₃

/--
Analytic predicate: this certified family exhausts the boundary-null test class.

This is the next real theorem target, not proved in Step 27.
-/
structure BoundaryNullExhaustive (F : DirectedCertFamily) : Prop where
  dense_boundary_null : Prop

/--
Analytic predicate: the Weil/PSD form is nonnegative on all boundary-null tests.

This is intentionally abstract at this layer. Later files connect it to the
actual `𝓦(h)` / explicit-formula object.
-/
structure BoundaryNullGlobalPositivity : Prop where
  holds : Prop

/--
Step 27 theorem shell.

If a directed certified family is boundary-null exhaustive, then it gives
global boundary-null positivity.

The actual analytic proof is postponed to the exhaustion layer.
-/
axiom boundaryNull_global_positivity_of_directed_cert_family
  (F : DirectedCertFamily)
  (hExh : BoundaryNullExhaustive F) :
  BoundaryNullGlobalPositivity

end PSDpd
end Q3
```

Да, здесь есть **axiom**. Это нормально для Step 27, если честно назвать его theorem-shell. Но лучше, если ты не хочешь добавлять новый axiom, сделать это как `structure`-поле, не как `axiom`.

Более чистый вариант без axioms:

```lean
structure DirectedFamilyExhaustionTheorem (F : DirectedCertFamily) where
  exhaustive : BoundaryNullExhaustive F
  globalPositivity : BoundaryNullGlobalPositivity
```

Я бы выбрал **без axiom**, чтобы не загрязнять axiom profile.

---

## 2. Рекомендованный Lean-файл без axioms

Лучше вот так:

```lean
import Q3.Proofs.PSD_PenaltyCertificate

namespace Q3
namespace PSDpd

structure FiniteSpaceLabel where
  id : String
  L : String
  ell : String
  delta : String
  kSpline : String
deriving Repr

structure CertifiedFiniteBlock where
  label : FiniteSpaceLabel
  cert : FinitePenaltyCert

class HasRefinement (α : Type) where
  Refines : α → α → Prop

structure DirectedCertFamily where
  Block : Type
  refinement : HasRefinement Block
  certBlock : Block → CertifiedFiniteBlock
  nonempty : ∃ b : Block, True
  directed :
    ∀ b₁ b₂ : Block,
      ∃ b₃ : Block,
        refinement.Refines b₁ b₃ ∧ refinement.Refines b₂ b₃

/--
This is the future analytic exhaustion statement.

It should eventually mean:
the finite spaces in `F` are dense in the boundary-null test class.
-/
structure BoundaryNullExhaustive (F : DirectedCertFamily) : Prop where
  dense_boundary_null : Prop

/--
This is the future analytic positivity output.

It should eventually mean:
for every compactly supported boundary-null test h,
the Weil/PSD quadratic form is nonnegative.
-/
structure BoundaryNullGlobalPositivity : Prop where
  holds : Prop

/--
A theorem package connecting a directed finite certificate family
to global boundary-null positivity.

This is a record, not an axiom.
-/
structure DirectedFamilyClosure (F : DirectedCertFamily) where
  exhaustive : BoundaryNullExhaustive F
  positivity : BoundaryNullGlobalPositivity

/--
Consumer theorem: if a closure package exists, expose its positivity.
-/
theorem boundaryNull_global_positivity_of_closure
    (F : DirectedCertFamily)
    (H : DirectedFamilyClosure F) :
    BoundaryNullGlobalPositivity :=
  H.positivity

end PSDpd
end Q3
```

**Почему так лучше:**  
никаких новых axioms, никаких `sorry`, только интерфейс.

# 3. Генератор из ledger в family seed

Создай скрипт:

```bash
q3.lean.aristotle/scripts/q3_psdpd_step27_family_seed.py
```

Он читает:

```bash
docs/insights/q3_psdpd_finitecert_ledger.json
```

и генерирует JSON:

```bash
docs/insights/q3_psdpd_directed_family_seed.json
```

Код:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Step 27 PSD-pd directed-family seed generator.

Reads Step 26 FiniteCert ledger JSON and emits a first directed-family seed.

This does not prove exhaustion. It creates the audit object that later
Step 28/29 will enrich into an actual certificate family.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path


def run() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument(
        "--ledger",
        default="q3.lean.aristotle/docs/insights/q3_psdpd_finitecert_ledger.json",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--out",
        default="q3.lean.aristotle/docs/insights/q3_psdpd_directed_family_seed.json",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()

    ledger_path = Path(args.ledger)
    if not ledger_path.exists():
        raise SystemExit(f"Ledger JSON not found: {ledger_path}")

    ledger = json.loads(ledger_path.read_text())
    blocks = ledger.get("blocks", [])

    family_blocks = []

    for b in blocks:
        family_blocks.append(
            {
                "block_id": b["block_id"],
                "family_id": b["family_id"],
                "role": b["role"],
                "L": b["L"],
                "k_spline": b["k_spline"],
                "ell": b["ell"],
                "delta": b["delta"],
                "kappa": b["kappa"],
                "theta": b["theta"],
                "dtheta_floor": {
                    "num": b["dtheta_floor_num"],
                    "den": b["dtheta_floor_den"],
                },
                "rkappa_floor": {
                    "num": b["rkappa_floor_num"],
                    "den": b["rkappa_floor_den"],
                },
                "midpoint_csv": b["midpoint_csv"],
                "radius_csv": b["radius_csv"],
                "midpoint_sha256": b["midpoint_sha256"],
                "radius_sha256": b["radius_sha256"],
            }
        )

    payload = {
        "schema": "q3_psdpd_directed_family_seed_v1",
        "status": "seed_only_not_exhaustive",
        "meaning": (
            "This seed records accepted finite certificate blocks. "
            "It is not yet an exhausting directed family."
        ),
        "next_required_theorems": [
            "directed_refinement_relation",
            "boundary_null_correction",
            "finite_space_density",
            "Weil_form_continuity",
            "uniform_certificate_family",
        ],
        "blocks": family_blocks,
    }

    out = Path(args.out)
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, sort_keys=True))

    print("== Step 27 directed-family seed ==")
    print(f"blocks accepted: {len(family_blocks)}")
    print(f"wrote: {out}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run()
```

# Step 28 — Boundary-null correction lemma

## 0. Что надо доказать

У нас есть два boundary functional:

\[
H(1/2),\qquad H(-1/2).
\]

Для теста \(h\):

\[
H(z)=\int h(u)e^{zu}\,du.
\]

Boundary-null condition:

\[
\boxed{
H(1/2)=0,\qquad H(-1/2)=0.
}
\]

Проблема: обычная approximation sequence \(g_n\to h\) не обязана сохранять эти два нуля.

Решение: выбрать два fixed corrector bumps:

\[
b_+,\ b_-,
\]

так, чтобы matrix

\[
M=
\begin{pmatrix}
B_+(1/2)&B_-(1/2)\\
B_+(-1/2)&B_-(-1/2)
\end{pmatrix}
\]

была invertible.

Тогда можно исправлять любой approximant:

\[
\boxed{
g_n^\circ
=
g_n-\lambda_{n,+}b_+-\lambda_{n,-}b_-,
}
\]

и получить:

\[
G_n^\circ(1/2)=G_n^\circ(-1/2)=0.
\]

**Boundary functional** — линейное отображение \(h\mapsto H(\pm 1/2)\).  
**Corrector bump** — фиксированный тест, которым мы исправляем boundary values.  
**Boundary-null correction** — процедура, которая превращает approximant в boundary-null approximant.  
**Invertible evaluation matrix** — условие, что два corrector bumps независимо управляют двумя boundary values.

# 1. Самый быстрый Lean-файл

Создать:

```bash
q3.lean.aristotle/Q3/Proofs/PSD_BoundaryNullCorrection.lean
```

Код:

```lean
import Q3.Proofs.PSD_CertificateFamily
import Mathlib.Tactic

namespace Q3
namespace PSDpd

/--
Two boundary linear functionals.

In the analytic model these are:
  h ↦ H(1/2)
  h ↦ H(-1/2)
but this file stays algebraic.
-/
structure BoundaryPair (V : Type*) [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V] where
  evalPlus : V →ₗ[ℝ] ℝ
  evalMinus : V →ₗ[ℝ] ℝ

/-- Determinant of the 2×2 boundary evaluation matrix on two correctors. -/
def boundaryDet
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V) (bPlus bMinus : V) : ℝ :=
  E.evalPlus bPlus * E.evalMinus bMinus
    - E.evalPlus bMinus * E.evalMinus bPlus

/--
Algebraic boundary correction.

If two corrector vectors have invertible boundary evaluation matrix, then every
vector can be corrected by a linear combination of them so that both boundary
functionals vanish.
-/
theorem boundary_correction_exists
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V) (bPlus bMinus g : V)
    (hdet : boundaryDet E bPlus bMinus ≠ 0) :
    ∃ aPlus aMinus : ℝ,
      E.evalPlus (g - aPlus • bPlus - aMinus • bMinus) = 0 ∧
      E.evalMinus (g - aPlus • bPlus - aMinus • bMinus) = 0 := by
  let A : ℝ := E.evalPlus bPlus
  let B : ℝ := E.evalPlus bMinus
  let C : ℝ := E.evalMinus bPlus
  let D : ℝ := E.evalMinus bMinus
  let p : ℝ := E.evalPlus g
  let q : ℝ := E.evalMinus g
  let Δ : ℝ := A * D - B * C

  have hΔ : Δ ≠ 0 := by
    simpa [boundaryDet, A, B, C, D, Δ] using hdet

  let aPlus : ℝ := (p * D - B * q) / Δ
  let aMinus : ℝ := (A * q - p * C) / Δ

  refine ⟨aPlus, aMinus, ?_, ?_⟩

  · calc
      E.evalPlus (g - aPlus • bPlus - aMinus • bMinus)
          = p - aPlus * A - aMinus * B := by
              simp [p, A, B, aPlus, aMinus]
      _ = 0 := by
              field_simp [aPlus, aMinus, Δ, hΔ]
              ring

  · calc
      E.evalMinus (g - aPlus • bPlus - aMinus • bMinus)
          = q - aPlus * C - aMinus * D := by
              simp [q, C, D, aPlus, aMinus]
      _ = 0 := by
              field_simp [aPlus, aMinus, Δ, hΔ]
              ring

/--
The future analytic meaning of a corrector pair.

This is intentionally only a structure, not a theorem:
the actual analytic construction of compactly supported smooth correctors
belongs to the exhaustion layer.
-/
structure BoundaryCorrectorData
    (V : Type*) [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V] where
  boundary : BoundaryPair V
  bPlus : V
  bMinus : V
  det_ne_zero : boundaryDet boundary bPlus bMinus ≠ 0

/--
Every `BoundaryCorrectorData` gives algebraic correction coefficients.
-/
theorem boundary_correction_from_data
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V]
    (D : BoundaryCorrectorData V) (g : V) :
    ∃ aPlus aMinus : ℝ,
      D.boundary.evalPlus (g - aPlus • D.bPlus - aMinus • D.bMinus) = 0 ∧
      D.boundary.evalMinus (g - aPlus • D.bPlus - aMinus • D.bMinus) = 0 :=
  boundary_correction_exists D.boundary D.bPlus D.bMinus g D.det_ne_zero

end PSDpd
end Q3
```

# 3. Почему это быстро и правильно

Step 28 не пытается сразу доказать всю density/exhaustion. Он закрывает **алгебраическое ядро**:

\[
\boxed{
\det M\ne0
\Rightarrow
\text{boundary correction exists}.
}
\]

Следующий analytic layer уже будет:

\[
g_n\to h,\quad h\text{ boundary-null}
\Rightarrow
a_{n,+}\to0,\quad a_{n,-}\to0.
\]

Почему коэффициенты уходят в ноль?

Потому что:

\[
E_+(h)=E_-(h)=0,
\]

а если \(g_n\to h\) и \(E_\pm\) continuous, то:

\[
E_\pm(g_n)\to0.
\]

Коэффициенты correction являются линейными выражениями от:

\[
E_+(g_n),\quad E_-(g_n),
\]

значит тоже стремятся к нулю.

Это будет Step 29.

Содержимое:

```md
# Step 28 — Boundary-null correction lemma

## Goal

Close the algebraic core needed for boundary-null exhaustion.

Given two boundary functionals

\[
E_+(h)=H(1/2),\qquad E_-(h)=H(-1/2),
\]

and two corrector bumps \(b_+,b_-\), if

\[
\det
\begin{pmatrix}
E_+(b_+) & E_+(b_-)\\
E_-(b_+) & E_-(b_-)
\end{pmatrix}
\ne0,
\]

then every approximant \(g\) can be corrected to

\[
g^\circ=g-a_+b_+-a_-b_-
\]

with

\[
E_+(g^\circ)=E_-(g^\circ)=0.
\]

## Lean file

`Q3/Proofs/PSD_BoundaryNullCorrection.lean`

## Main theorem

`boundary_correction_exists`

## Why this matters

Step 23 requires density inside the boundary-null test class.
Ordinary finite-space density does not preserve \(H(\pm1/2)=0\).
This lemma gives the algebraic correction mechanism.

## What is still missing

The analytic exhaustion layer must prove:

1. existence of compactly supported smooth correctors \(b_+,b_-\);
2. continuity of \(E_\pm\) in the selected topology;
3. if \(g_n\to h\) and \(h\) is boundary-null, then the correction coefficients go to zero;
4. the corrected sequence stays in the directed finite-family closure.

## Verdict

Step 28 closes the algebraic boundary-null correction core.
Step 29 should prove the small-coefficient / convergence version.
```

\exists a_+,a_-:
E_+(g-a_+b_+-a_-b_-)=0,\quad
E_-(g-a_+b_+-a_-b_-)=0.
\]

Теперь надо доказать:

\[
g_n\to h,\quad E_+(h)=E_-(h)=0
\Rightarrow
a_{n,+}\to0,\quad a_{n,-}\to0,
\]

и поэтому:

\[
g_n^\circ
=
g_n-a_{n,+}b_+-a_{n,-}b_-
\to h.
\]

Это ровно bridge от обычной density к **boundary-null density**. Текущий `INSIGHTS.md` уже фиксирует, что Step 28 закрыл алгебраическое ядро, а следующий blocker — analytic small-coefficient convergence. fileciteturn3file0

# Step 29 — Boundary correction convergence

## 1. Lean-файл

Создай:

```bash
q3.lean.aristotle/Q3/Proofs/PSD_BoundaryNullConvergence.lean
```

Код:

```lean
import Q3.Proofs.PSD_BoundaryNullCorrection
import Mathlib.Analysis.Normed.Module.Basic
import Mathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.Basic
import Mathlib.Tactic

noncomputable section

open Filter
open scoped Topology

namespace Q3
namespace PSDpd

/--
Explicit coefficient correcting the `evalPlus` boundary value.

For boundary matrix

  A = E₊(b₊), B = E₊(b₋)
  C = E₋(b₊), D = E₋(b₋)

and p = E₊(g), q = E₋(g), this is

  a₊ = (pD - Bq) / det.
-/
def boundaryCoeffPlus
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V) (bPlus bMinus g : V) : ℝ :=
  (E.evalPlus g * E.evalMinus bMinus
    - E.evalPlus bMinus * E.evalMinus g)
    / boundaryDet E bPlus bMinus

/--
Explicit coefficient correcting the `evalMinus` boundary value.

  a₋ = (Aq - pC) / det.
-/
def boundaryCoeffMinus
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V) (bPlus bMinus g : V) : ℝ :=
  (E.evalPlus bPlus * E.evalMinus g
    - E.evalPlus g * E.evalMinus bPlus)
    / boundaryDet E bPlus bMinus

/-- The explicitly corrected vector. -/
def boundaryCorrected
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V) (bPlus bMinus g : V) : V :=
  g
    - boundaryCoeffPlus E bPlus bMinus g • bPlus
    - boundaryCoeffMinus E bPlus bMinus g • bMinus

/-- The explicit correction kills the plus boundary. -/
theorem boundaryCorrected_evalPlus_zero
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V) (bPlus bMinus g : V)
    (hdet : boundaryDet E bPlus bMinus ≠ 0) :
    E.evalPlus (boundaryCorrected E bPlus bMinus g) = 0 := by
  simp [boundaryCorrected, boundaryCoeffPlus, boundaryCoeffMinus, boundaryDet]
  field_simp [hdet]
  ring

/-- The explicit correction kills the minus boundary. -/
theorem boundaryCorrected_evalMinus_zero
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V) (bPlus bMinus g : V)
    (hdet : boundaryDet E bPlus bMinus ≠ 0) :
    E.evalMinus (boundaryCorrected E bPlus bMinus g) = 0 := by
  simp [boundaryCorrected, boundaryCoeffPlus, boundaryCoeffMinus, boundaryDet]
  field_simp [hdet]
  ring

/--
If both boundary values of `gₙ` tend to zero, then the plus correction
coefficient tends to zero.
-/
theorem boundaryCoeffPlus_tendsto_zero
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V) (bPlus bMinus : V) (g : ℕ → V)
    (hPlus : Tendsto (fun n => E.evalPlus (g n)) atTop (nhds 0))
    (hMinus : Tendsto (fun n => E.evalMinus (g n)) atTop (nhds 0)) :
    Tendsto
      (fun n => boundaryCoeffPlus E bPlus bMinus (g n))
      atTop
      (nhds 0) := by
  let B : ℝ := E.evalPlus bMinus
  let D : ℝ := E.evalMinus bMinus
  let Δ : ℝ := boundaryDet E bPlus bMinus

  have hpair :
      Tendsto
        (fun n => ((E.evalPlus (g n), E.evalMinus (g n)) : ℝ × ℝ))
        atTop
        (nhds ((0, 0) : ℝ × ℝ)) :=
    hPlus.prod hMinus

  have hcont :
      Continuous
        (fun x : ℝ × ℝ => (x.1 * D - B * x.2) / Δ) := by
    continuity

  have hres :=
    (hcont.tendsto ((0, 0) : ℝ × ℝ)).comp hpair

  simpa [boundaryCoeffPlus, B, D, Δ] using hres

/--
If both boundary values of `gₙ` tend to zero, then the minus correction
coefficient tends to zero.
-/
theorem boundaryCoeffMinus_tendsto_zero
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V) (bPlus bMinus : V) (g : ℕ → V)
    (hPlus : Tendsto (fun n => E.evalPlus (g n)) atTop (nhds 0))
    (hMinus : Tendsto (fun n => E.evalMinus (g n)) atTop (nhds 0)) :
    Tendsto
      (fun n => boundaryCoeffMinus E bPlus bMinus (g n))
      atTop
      (nhds 0) := by
  let A : ℝ := E.evalPlus bPlus
  let C : ℝ := E.evalMinus bPlus
  let Δ : ℝ := boundaryDet E bPlus bMinus

  have hpair :
      Tendsto
        (fun n => ((E.evalPlus (g n), E.evalMinus (g n)) : ℝ × ℝ))
        atTop
        (nhds ((0, 0) : ℝ × ℝ)) :=
    hPlus.prod hMinus

  have hcont :
      Continuous
        (fun x : ℝ × ℝ => (A * x.2 - x.1 * C) / Δ) := by
    continuity

  have hres :=
    (hcont.tendsto ((0, 0) : ℝ × ℝ)).comp hpair

  simpa [boundaryCoeffMinus, A, C, Δ] using hres

/--
If `gₙ → h` and the boundary values of `gₙ` tend to zero, then the explicitly
corrected approximants still converge to `h`.
-/
theorem boundaryCorrected_tendsto
    {V : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup V] [NormedSpace ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V) (bPlus bMinus h : V) (g : ℕ → V)
    (hg : Tendsto g atTop (nhds h))
    (hPlus : Tendsto (fun n => E.evalPlus (g n)) atTop (nhds 0))
    (hMinus : Tendsto (fun n => E.evalMinus (g n)) atTop (nhds 0)) :
    Tendsto
      (fun n => boundaryCorrected E bPlus bMinus (g n))
      atTop
      (nhds h) := by
  have haPlus :
      Tendsto
        (fun n => boundaryCoeffPlus E bPlus bMinus (g n))
        atTop
        (nhds 0) :=
    boundaryCoeffPlus_tendsto_zero E bPlus bMinus g hPlus hMinus

  have haMinus :
      Tendsto
        (fun n => boundaryCoeffMinus E bPlus bMinus (g n))
        atTop
        (nhds 0) :=
    boundaryCoeffMinus_tendsto_zero E bPlus bMinus g hPlus hMinus

  have htriple :
      Tendsto
        (fun n =>
          (g n,
            (boundaryCoeffPlus E bPlus bMinus (g n),
             boundaryCoeffMinus E bPlus bMinus (g n))))
        atTop
        (nhds (h, (0, 0))) :=
    hg.prod (haPlus.prod haMinus)

  have hcont :
      Continuous
        (fun x : V × (ℝ × ℝ) =>
          x.1 - x.2.1 • bPlus - x.2.2 • bMinus) := by
    continuity

  have hres :=
    (hcont.tendsto (h, (0, 0))).comp htriple

  simpa [boundaryCorrected] using hres

/--
If boundary functionals are continuous and `gₙ → h` with `h` boundary-null,
then the corrected approximants converge to `h`.
-/
theorem boundaryCorrected_tendsto_of_continuous_boundary
    {V : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup V] [NormedSpace ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V) (bPlus bMinus h : V) (g : ℕ → V)
    (hEvalPlus_cont : Continuous E.evalPlus)
    (hEvalMinus_cont : Continuous E.evalMinus)
    (hg : Tendsto g atTop (nhds h))
    (hPlus_h : E.evalPlus h = 0)
    (hMinus_h : E.evalMinus h = 0) :
    Tendsto
      (fun n => boundaryCorrected E bPlus bMinus (g n))
      atTop
      (nhds h) := by
  have hPlus :
      Tendsto (fun n => E.evalPlus (g n)) atTop (nhds 0) := by
    have htmp := (hEvalPlus_cont.tendsto h).comp hg
    simpa [hPlus_h] using htmp

  have hMinus :
      Tendsto (fun n => E.evalMinus (g n)) atTop (nhds 0) := by
    have htmp := (hEvalMinus_cont.tendsto h).comp hg
    simpa [hMinus_h] using htmp

  exact boundaryCorrected_tendsto E bPlus bMinus h g hg hPlus hMinus

end PSDpd
end Q3
```

Step 28 дал:

\[
\det M\ne0
\Rightarrow
g\mapsto g^\circ
\text{ можно сделать boundary-null}.
\]

Step 29 даёт:

\[
g_n\to h,\quad h\in\ker(E_+,E_-)
\Rightarrow
g_n^\circ\to h.
\]

То есть теперь можно честно писать:

\[
\boxed{
\text{ordinary dense finite approximants}
\Rightarrow
\text{boundary-null dense finite approximants}.
}
\]

Это уже прямой вход в Step 30: **Boundary-null exhaustion theorem**.

```md
# Step 29 — Boundary-null correction convergence

## Goal

Upgrade the algebraic correction from Step 28 to a convergence statement.

If

\[
g_n\to h,\qquad E_+(h)=E_-(h)=0,
\]

and \(E_\pm\) are continuous, then the corrected approximants

\[
g_n^\circ
=
g_n-a_{n,+}b_+-a_{n,-}b_-
\]

still satisfy

\[
g_n^\circ\to h.
\]

## Lean file

`Q3/Proofs/PSD_BoundaryNullConvergence.lean`

## Main definitions

- `boundaryCoeffPlus`
- `boundaryCoeffMinus`
- `boundaryCorrected`

## Main theorems

- `boundaryCorrected_evalPlus_zero`
- `boundaryCorrected_evalMinus_zero`
- `boundaryCoeffPlus_tendsto_zero`
- `boundaryCoeffMinus_tendsto_zero`
- `boundaryCorrected_tendsto`
- `boundaryCorrected_tendsto_of_continuous_boundary`

## Meaning

Step 28 proves the correction exists.

Step 29 proves the correction is asymptotically harmless.

Together they supply the boundary-null density hinge needed by the Step 23
exhaustion contract.

## Next blocker

Step 30: boundary-null exhaustion theorem.

This should combine:

1. ordinary finite-space density;
2. continuity of boundary functionals;
3. Step 28 algebraic correction;
4. Step 29 convergence of corrected approximants.
```

```lean
import Q3.Proofs.PSD_BoundaryNullCorrection
import Mathlib.Analysis.Normed.Module.Basic
import Mathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.Basic
import Mathlib.Tactic

noncomputable section

open Filter
open scoped Topology

namespace Q3
namespace PSDpd

/--
Explicit coefficient correcting the `evalPlus` boundary value.

For boundary matrix

  A = E₊(b₊), B = E₊(b₋)
  C = E₋(b₊), D = E₋(b₋)

and p = E₊(g), q = E₋(g), this is

  a₊ = (pD - Bq) / det.
-/
def boundaryCoeffPlus
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V) (bPlus bMinus g : V) : ℝ :=
  (E.evalPlus g * E.evalMinus bMinus
    - E.evalPlus bMinus * E.evalMinus g)
    / boundaryDet E bPlus bMinus

/--
Explicit coefficient correcting the `evalMinus` boundary value.

  a₋ = (Aq - pC) / det.
-/
def boundaryCoeffMinus
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V) (bPlus bMinus g : V) : ℝ :=
  (E.evalPlus bPlus * E.evalMinus g
    - E.evalPlus g * E.evalMinus bPlus)
    / boundaryDet E bPlus bMinus

/-- The explicitly corrected vector. -/
def boundaryCorrected
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V) (bPlus bMinus g : V) : V :=
  g
    - boundaryCoeffPlus E bPlus bMinus g • bPlus
    - boundaryCoeffMinus E bPlus bMinus g • bMinus

/-- The explicit correction kills the plus boundary. -/
theorem boundaryCorrected_evalPlus_zero
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V) (bPlus bMinus g : V)
    (hdet : boundaryDet E bPlus bMinus ≠ 0) :
    E.evalPlus (boundaryCorrected E bPlus bMinus g) = 0 := by
  simp [boundaryCorrected, boundaryCoeffPlus, boundaryCoeffMinus, boundaryDet]
  field_simp [hdet]
  ring

/-- The explicit correction kills the minus boundary. -/
theorem boundaryCorrected_evalMinus_zero
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V) (bPlus bMinus g : V)
    (hdet : boundaryDet E bPlus bMinus ≠ 0) :
    E.evalMinus (boundaryCorrected E bPlus bMinus g) = 0 := by
  simp [boundaryCorrected, boundaryCoeffPlus, boundaryCoeffMinus, boundaryDet]
  field_simp [hdet]
  ring

/--
If both boundary values of `gₙ` tend to zero, then the plus correction
coefficient tends to zero.
-/
theorem boundaryCoeffPlus_tendsto_zero
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V) (bPlus bMinus : V) (g : ℕ → V)
    (hPlus : Tendsto (fun n => E.evalPlus (g n)) atTop (nhds 0))
    (hMinus : Tendsto (fun n => E.evalMinus (g n)) atTop (nhds 0)) :
    Tendsto
      (fun n => boundaryCoeffPlus E bPlus bMinus (g n))
      atTop
      (nhds 0) := by
  let B : ℝ := E.evalPlus bMinus
  let D : ℝ := E.evalMinus bMinus
  let Δ : ℝ := boundaryDet E bPlus bMinus

  have hpair :
      Tendsto
        (fun n => ((E.evalPlus (g n), E.evalMinus (g n)) : ℝ × ℝ))
        atTop
        (nhds ((0, 0) : ℝ × ℝ)) :=
    hPlus.prod hMinus

  have hcont :
      Continuous
        (fun x : ℝ × ℝ => (x.1 * D - B * x.2) / Δ) := by
    continuity

  have hres :=
    (hcont.tendsto ((0, 0) : ℝ × ℝ)).comp hpair

  simpa [boundaryCoeffPlus, B, D, Δ] using hres

/--
If both boundary values of `gₙ` tend to zero, then the minus correction
coefficient tends to zero.
-/
theorem boundaryCoeffMinus_tendsto_zero
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V) (bPlus bMinus : V) (g : ℕ → V)
    (hPlus : Tendsto (fun n => E.evalPlus (g n)) atTop (nhds 0))
    (hMinus : Tendsto (fun n => E.evalMinus (g n)) atTop (nhds 0)) :
    Tendsto
      (fun n => boundaryCoeffMinus E bPlus bMinus (g n))
      atTop
      (nhds 0) := by
  let A : ℝ := E.evalPlus bPlus
  let C : ℝ := E.evalMinus bPlus
  let Δ : ℝ := boundaryDet E bPlus bMinus

  have hpair :
      Tendsto
        (fun n => ((E.evalPlus (g n), E.evalMinus (g n)) : ℝ × ℝ))
        atTop
        (nhds ((0, 0) : ℝ × ℝ)) :=
    hPlus.prod hMinus

  have hcont :
      Continuous
        (fun x : ℝ × ℝ => (A * x.2 - x.1 * C) / Δ) := by
    continuity

  have hres :=
    (hcont.tendsto ((0, 0) : ℝ × ℝ)).comp hpair

  simpa [boundaryCoeffMinus, A, C, Δ] using hres

/--
If `gₙ → h` and the boundary values of `gₙ` tend to zero, then the explicitly
corrected approximants still converge to `h`.
-/
theorem boundaryCorrected_tendsto
    {V : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup V] [NormedSpace ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V) (bPlus bMinus h : V) (g : ℕ → V)
    (hg : Tendsto g atTop (nhds h))
    (hPlus : Tendsto (fun n => E.evalPlus (g n)) atTop (nhds 0))
    (hMinus : Tendsto (fun n => E.evalMinus (g n)) atTop (nhds 0)) :
    Tendsto
      (fun n => boundaryCorrected E bPlus bMinus (g n))
      atTop
      (nhds h) := by
  have haPlus :
      Tendsto
        (fun n => boundaryCoeffPlus E bPlus bMinus (g n))
        atTop
        (nhds 0) :=
    boundaryCoeffPlus_tendsto_zero E bPlus bMinus g hPlus hMinus

  have haMinus :
      Tendsto
        (fun n => boundaryCoeffMinus E bPlus bMinus (g n))
        atTop
        (nhds 0) :=
    boundaryCoeffMinus_tendsto_zero E bPlus bMinus g hPlus hMinus

  have hpairCoeff :
      Tendsto
        (fun n =>
          ((boundaryCoeffPlus E bPlus bMinus (g n),
            boundaryCoeffMinus E bPlus bMinus (g n)) : ℝ × ℝ))
        atTop
        (nhds ((0, 0) : ℝ × ℝ)) :=
    haPlus.prod haMinus

  have htriple :
      Tendsto
        (fun n =>
          ((g n,
            (boundaryCoeffPlus E bPlus bMinus (g n),
             boundaryCoeffMinus E bPlus bMinus (g n))) : V × (ℝ × ℝ)))
        atTop
        (nhds ((h, (0, 0)) : V × (ℝ × ℝ))) :=
    hg.prod hpairCoeff

  have hcont :
      Continuous
        (fun x : V × (ℝ × ℝ) =>
          x.1 - x.2.1 • bPlus - x.2.2 • bMinus) := by
    continuity

  have hres :=
    (hcont.tendsto ((h, (0, 0)) : V × (ℝ × ℝ))).comp htriple

  simpa [boundaryCorrected] using hres

/--
If boundary functionals are continuous and `gₙ → h` with `h` boundary-null,
then the corrected approximants converge to `h`.
-/
theorem boundaryCorrected_tendsto_of_continuous_boundary
    {V : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup V] [NormedSpace ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V) (bPlus bMinus h : V) (g : ℕ → V)
    (hEvalPlus_cont : Continuous E.evalPlus)
    (hEvalMinus_cont : Continuous E.evalMinus)
    (hg : Tendsto g atTop (nhds h))
    (hPlus_h : E.evalPlus h = 0)
    (hMinus_h : E.evalMinus h = 0) :
    Tendsto
      (fun n => boundaryCorrected E bPlus bMinus (g n))
      atTop
      (nhds h) := by
  have hPlus :
      Tendsto (fun n => E.evalPlus (g n)) atTop (nhds 0) := by
    have htmp := (hEvalPlus_cont.tendsto h).comp hg
    simpa [hPlus_h] using htmp

  have hMinus :
      Tendsto (fun n => E.evalMinus (g n)) atTop (nhds 0) := by
    have htmp := (hEvalMinus_cont.tendsto h).comp hg
    simpa [hMinus_h] using htmp

  exact boundaryCorrected_tendsto E bPlus bMinus h g hg hPlus hMinus

end PSDpd
end Q3
```

# Что Step 29 закрывает

Теперь связка такая:

\[
\boxed{
\textbf{Step 28: correction exists}
}
\]

\[
\boxed{
\textbf{Step 29: correction converges harmlessly}
}
\]

То есть:

\[
g_n\to h,\quad h\in\ker(E_+,E_-)
\]

даёт:

\[
g_n^\circ\to h,\quad g_n^\circ\in\ker(E_+,E_-).
\]

Это ровно механизм для **boundary-null exhaustion**.

# Step 30 — Boundary-null exhaustion theorem

Теперь собираем Step 28 + Step 29 в один theorem-shell:

\[
\boxed{
\text{ordinary finite density}
+
\text{boundary correction stability}
\Rightarrow
\text{boundary-null density}
}
\]

Это ровно следующий мост к Step 23: finite certificates должны покрывать не просто произвольные approximants, а approximants с условиями:

\[
H(1/2)=H(-1/2)=0.
\]

Текущий `INSIGHTS.md` уже фиксирует, что после Step 28/29 следующий blocker — **boundary-null exhaustion**. fileciteturn3file0

**Boundary-null exhaustion** — плотность finite approximants внутри класса тестов, где оба boundary functional равны нулю.

**Ordinary density** — обычное приближение \(g_n\to h\) без сохранения boundary-условий.

**Correction stability** — свойство family: если \(g\) лежит в family, то исправленный \(g^\circ\) тоже лежит в family.

# Step 30 Lean file

Создай:


Код:

```lean
import Q3.Proofs.PSD_BoundaryNullConvergence
import Q3.Proofs.PSD_CertificateFamily
import Mathlib.Analysis.Normed.Module.Basic
import Mathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.Basic
import Mathlib.Tactic

noncomputable section

open Filter
open scoped Topology

namespace Q3
namespace PSDpd

/--
A sequence-level ordinary dense approximation property.

`InFamily` is an abstract predicate representing the union of finite certified
spaces or a directed finite-family closure.
-/
def OrdinaryDense
    {V : Type*} [TopologicalSpace V]
    (InFamily : V → Prop) : Prop :=
  ∀ h : V,
    ∃ g : ℕ → V,
      (∀ n, InFamily (g n)) ∧
      Tendsto g atTop (nhds h)

/--
Boundary-null density: every boundary-null target has a convergent sequence
inside the family, and every approximant is itself boundary-null.
-/
def BoundaryNullDense
    {V : Type*} [TopologicalSpace V] [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V)
    (InFamily : V → Prop) : Prop :=
  ∀ h : V,
    E.evalPlus h = 0 →
    E.evalMinus h = 0 →
    ∃ g : ℕ → V,
      (∀ n, InFamily (g n)) ∧
      (∀ n, E.evalPlus (g n) = 0 ∧ E.evalMinus (g n) = 0) ∧
      Tendsto g atTop (nhds h)

/--
The correction operation is stable inside the chosen finite-family closure.
-/
def BoundaryCorrectionStable
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V)
    (bPlus bMinus : V)
    (InFamily : V → Prop) : Prop :=
  ∀ g : V,
    InFamily g →
    InFamily (boundaryCorrected E bPlus bMinus g)

/--
Core Step 30 theorem.

If:
1. the family is ordinarily dense;
2. boundary functionals are continuous;
3. two correctors have nonzero boundary determinant;
4. correction preserves membership in the family;

then the family is dense inside the boundary-null subspace.
-/
theorem boundaryNullDense_of_ordinaryDense_and_correctionStable
    {V : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup V] [NormedSpace ℝ V]
    (E : BoundaryPair V)
    (bPlus bMinus : V)
    (InFamily : V → Prop)
    (hDense : OrdinaryDense InFamily)
    (hStable : BoundaryCorrectionStable E bPlus bMinus InFamily)
    (hdet : boundaryDet E bPlus bMinus ≠ 0)
    (hEvalPlus_cont : Continuous E.evalPlus)
    (hEvalMinus_cont : Continuous E.evalMinus) :
    BoundaryNullDense E InFamily := by
  intro h hPlus_h hMinus_h

  obtain ⟨g, hgIn, hgTendsto⟩ := hDense h

  let gcorr : ℕ → V :=
    fun n => boundaryCorrected E bPlus bMinus (g n)

  refine ⟨gcorr, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩

  · intro n
    exact hStable (g n) (hgIn n)

  · intro n
    constructor
    · exact boundaryCorrected_evalPlus_zero E bPlus bMinus (g n) hdet
    · exact boundaryCorrected_evalMinus_zero E bPlus bMinus (g n) hdet

  · exact
      boundaryCorrected_tendsto_of_continuous_boundary
        E bPlus bMinus h g
        hEvalPlus_cont hEvalMinus_cont
        hgTendsto hPlus_h hMinus_h

/--
A package for the Step 30 boundary-null exhaustion bridge.

This is useful as a theorem-facing object for Step 23 / directed-family closure.
-/
structure BoundaryNullExhaustionPackage
    (V : Type*) [NormedAddCommGroup V] [NormedSpace ℝ V] where
  boundary : BoundaryPair V
  bPlus : V
  bMinus : V
  InFamily : V → Prop

  ordinaryDense : OrdinaryDense InFamily
  correctionStable : BoundaryCorrectionStable boundary bPlus bMinus InFamily

  det_ne_zero : boundaryDet boundary bPlus bMinus ≠ 0
  evalPlus_cont : Continuous boundary.evalPlus
  evalMinus_cont : Continuous boundary.evalMinus

/--
A packaged boundary-null exhaustion theorem.
-/
theorem BoundaryNullExhaustionPackage.boundaryNullDense
    {V : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup V] [NormedSpace ℝ V]
    (P : BoundaryNullExhaustionPackage V) :
    BoundaryNullDense P.boundary P.InFamily :=
  boundaryNullDense_of_ordinaryDense_and_correctionStable
    P.boundary
    P.bPlus
    P.bMinus
    P.InFamily
    P.ordinaryDense
    P.correctionStable
    P.det_ne_zero
    P.evalPlus_cont
    P.evalMinus_cont

/--
Abstract bridge into the directed-family skeleton.

This keeps Step 30 independent of the concrete analytic realization of the
directed family. Later files can instantiate `InFamily` as the union/closure of
certified finite blocks.
-/
structure DirectedBoundaryNullExhaustion
    (F : DirectedCertFamily)
    (V : Type*) [NormedAddCommGroup V] [NormedSpace ℝ V] where
  package : BoundaryNullExhaustionPackage V

/--
Consumer theorem: a directed-family boundary-null exhaustion package yields
boundary-null density for its underlying analytic family predicate.
-/
theorem directedBoundaryNullDense
    {V : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup V] [NormedSpace ℝ V]
    {F : DirectedCertFamily}
    (D : DirectedBoundaryNullExhaustion F V) :
    BoundaryNullDense D.package.boundary D.package.InFamily :=
  D.package.boundaryNullDense

end PSDpd
end Q3
```

+

# Step 31 — Matrix identification

## Goal

Создать theorem-facing слой:

```text
spline packet coordinates v
→ analytic packet h_v
→ WeilForm(h_v)
→ matrix quadratic form vᵀ C v
```

где:

\[
C=A-P.
\]

И отдельно:

\[
Qv=0
\iff
H_v(1/2)=H_v(-1/2)=0.
\]

**Matrix identification** — доказательство, что аналитическая форма на функциях совпадает с матрицей, построенной в certificate pipeline.

**Spline packet space** — конечномерное пространство тестов, натянутое на basis-функции \(\psi_j\).

**Weil form** — аналитическая квадратичная форма \(\mathcal W(h)=\mathcal A(h)+\mathcal B(h)-\mathcal P(h)\).

**Coordinate vector** — вектор \(v\), задающий тест:

\[
h_v=\sum_j v_j\psi_j.
\]

# What to add in Lean

Создай файл:

```bash
q3.lean.aristotle/Q3/Proofs/PSD_MatrixIdentification.lean
```

Лучше пока сделать **theorem-facing abstract receiver**, без новых аксиом и без pretending, что аналитические интегралы уже формализованы в Lean.

```lean
import Q3.Proofs.PSD_PenaltyCertificate
import Q3.Proofs.PSD_CertificateFamily
import Q3.Proofs.PSD_BoundaryNullExhaustion
import Mathlib.LinearAlgebra.Matrix.Symmetric
import Mathlib.Data.Matrix.Basic
import Mathlib.Tactic

noncomputable section

namespace Q3
namespace PSDpd

/--
Abstract finite packet model.

`Coord` is the finite coordinate type.
`Test` is the analytic test-function type.
`packet v` is the analytic test associated to coordinates `v`.
-/
structure FinitePacketModel where
  Coord : Type
  coordFintype : Fintype Coord
  coordDecEq : DecidableEq Coord
  Test : Type
  packet : (Coord → ℝ) → Test

attribute [instance] FinitePacketModel.coordFintype
attribute [instance] FinitePacketModel.coordDecEq

/--
Analytic data associated to a finite packet model.

`weilForm` is the analytic Weil form.
`boundaryPlus` and `boundaryMinus` are the two boundary functionals.
-/
structure FinitePacketAnalyticData (M : FinitePacketModel) where
  weilForm : M.Test → ℝ
  boundaryPlus : M.Test → ℝ
  boundaryMinus : M.Test → ℝ

/--
Matrix data associated to a finite packet model.

`C` is the full Weil matrix, intended as `A - P`.
`Qplus`, `Qminus` are the boundary rows.
-/
structure FinitePacketMatrixData (M : FinitePacketModel) where
  C : Matrix M.Coord M.Coord ℝ
  Qplus : M.Coord → ℝ
  Qminus : M.Coord → ℝ

/--
Quadratic form of a matrix on coordinates.
-/
def matrixQF {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (C : Matrix ι ι ℝ) (v : ι → ℝ) : ℝ :=
  ∑ i, ∑ j, v i * C i j * v j

/--
Boundary row evaluation.
-/
def rowEval {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (q : ι → ℝ) (v : ι → ℝ) : ℝ :=
  ∑ i, q i * v i

/--
Matrix identification package.

This is the exact bridge needed after Step 26:
the interval-backed matrix certificate applies to the analytic Weil form.
-/
structure MatrixIdentifiesWeil
    (M : FinitePacketModel)
    (A : FinitePacketAnalyticData M)
    (D : FinitePacketMatrixData M) : Prop where
  weil_eq_matrix :
    ∀ v : M.Coord → ℝ,
      A.weilForm (M.packet v) = matrixQF D.C v
  boundaryPlus_eq_row :
    ∀ v : M.Coord → ℝ,
      A.boundaryPlus (M.packet v) = rowEval D.Qplus v
  boundaryMinus_eq_row :
    ∀ v : M.Coord → ℝ,
      A.boundaryMinus (M.packet v) = rowEval D.Qminus v

/--
Coordinate-level boundary-null condition.
-/
def MatrixBoundaryNull
    {M : FinitePacketModel}
    (D : FinitePacketMatrixData M)
    (v : M.Coord → ℝ) : Prop :=
  rowEval D.Qplus v = 0 ∧ rowEval D.Qminus v = 0

/--
Analytic boundary-null condition.
-/
def AnalyticBoundaryNull
    {M : FinitePacketModel}
    (A : FinitePacketAnalyticData M)
    (h : M.Test) : Prop :=
  A.boundaryPlus h = 0 ∧ A.boundaryMinus h = 0

/--
Matrix identification transfers coordinate boundary-null to analytic boundary-null.
-/
theorem analytic_boundaryNull_of_matrix_boundaryNull
    {M : FinitePacketModel}
    {A : FinitePacketAnalyticData M}
    {D : FinitePacketMatrixData M}
    (Hid : MatrixIdentifiesWeil M A D)
    (v : M.Coord → ℝ)
    (hQ : MatrixBoundaryNull D v) :
    AnalyticBoundaryNull A (M.packet v) := by
  constructor
  · rw [Hid.boundaryPlus_eq_row]
    exact hQ.1
  · rw [Hid.boundaryMinus_eq_row]
    exact hQ.2

/--
Coordinate nonnegativity transfers to analytic Weil positivity on packets.
-/
theorem analytic_weil_nonneg_of_matrix_nonneg
    {M : FinitePacketModel}
    {A : FinitePacketAnalyticData M}
    {D : FinitePacketMatrixData M}
    (Hid : MatrixIdentifiesWeil M A D)
    (hC_nonneg : ∀ v : M.Coord → ℝ, 0 ≤ matrixQF D.C v)
    (v : M.Coord → ℝ) :
    0 ≤ A.weilForm (M.packet v) := by
  rw [Hid.weil_eq_matrix]
  exact hC_nonneg v

/--
Boundary-null finite positivity receiver.

This is the exact consumer shape for `FinitePenaltyCert`.
-/
structure FiniteWeilPositivityOnBoundaryNull
    (M : FinitePacketModel)
    (A : FinitePacketAnalyticData M) : Prop where
  nonneg :
    ∀ v : M.Coord → ℝ,
      AnalyticBoundaryNull A (M.packet v) →
      0 ≤ A.weilForm (M.packet v)

/--
Matrix nonnegativity on the coordinate boundary-null subspace implies analytic
finite Weil positivity on the analytic boundary-null packet subspace.
-/
theorem finiteWeilPositivity_of_matrixIdentification
    {M : FinitePacketModel}
    {A : FinitePacketAnalyticData M}
    {D : FinitePacketMatrixData M}
    (Hid : MatrixIdentifiesWeil M A D)
    (hC_boundary_nonneg :
      ∀ v : M.Coord → ℝ,
        MatrixBoundaryNull D v →
        0 ≤ matrixQF D.C v) :
    FiniteWeilPositivityOnBoundaryNull M A := by
  constructor
  intro v hAnalytic
  rw [Hid.weil_eq_matrix]
  apply hC_boundary_nonneg
  constructor
  · rw [← Hid.boundaryPlus_eq_row]
    exact hAnalytic.1
  · rw [← Hid.boundaryMinus_eq_row]
    exact hAnalytic.2

end PSDpd
end Q3
```

# Why this is the right bridge

We already have:

```text
FinitePenaltyCert
→ Dtheta/Rkappa penalty guard
→ C nonnegative on coordinate ker Q
```

Now Step 31 adds:

```text
coordinate ker Q
= analytic boundary-null packet condition
```

and:

```text
vᵀ C v
= WeilForm(h_v)
```

Therefore:

\[
\boxed{
\texttt{FinitePenaltyCert}
+
\texttt{MatrixIdentifiesWeil}
\Rightarrow
\texttt{FiniteWeilPositivityOnBoundaryNull}.
}
\]

That is the exact connection missing between the certificate backend and the analytic route.

---

# What remains after Step 31

After Step 31, the next true theorem-target is no longer “does the matrix certificate mean anything?”

It will mean something.

The next target becomes:

```text
Step 32 — instantiate MatrixIdentifiesWeil for the B-spline packet model.
```

That one is the real analytic identification:

\[
A_{ij}
=
\mathcal A(\psi_j,\psi_i),
\]

\[
P_{ij}
=
\mathcal P(\psi_j,\psi_i),
\]

\[
Q_{\pm,j}
=
H_j(\pm1/2).
\]

**Step 31** gives the abstract Lean receiver.  
**Step 32** proves the receiver for the actual spline packet formulas.

## Что уже проверено

У нас уже есть нормальная связка:

\[
\boxed{
A,\ P,\ P_0,\ Q
}
\]

сидят на **interval contract**.

**Interval contract** — это когда каждый элемент матрицы имеет строгий формат:

\[
\text{midpoint} \pm \text{radius}.
\]

То есть мы не просто “посчитали float-числа”, а задали проверяемые интервалы.

Дальше:

\[
D_\theta+\tau Q^TQ\succ0,
\qquad
R_\kappa+\tau Q^TQ\succ0.
\]

Это уже даёт **finite boundary-null positivity** через **penalty guard**.

**Penalty guard** — трюк, где мы не сертифицируем численный базис \(\ker Q\), а добавляем штраф \(\tau Q^TQ\). Если полная матрица положительна, то на \(\ker Q\) нужная форма тоже положительна.

Этот слой уже реально крепкий. По текущему `INSIGHTS.md`, PSD-pd линия как раз зафиксирована как finite-certificate backend: `A, P, P0, Q` уже приведены к interval-backed finite block, дальше строится family/exhaustion bridge. fileciteturn3file0

\[
\boxed{
\text{сертифицированная матрица}
\neq
\text{автоматически аналитическая WeilForm}
}
\]

Нужно доказать:

\[
\boxed{
\mathcal W(h_v)=v^T(A-P)v.
}
\]

И:

\[
\boxed{
Qv=(H_v(1/2),H_v(-1/2)).
}
\]

Вот это и есть **Matrix Identification**.

**Matrix Identification** — доказательство, что матрицы \(A,P,Q\), которые мы сертифицировали численно/интервально, действительно являются матрицами аналитической формы на выбранном basis.

Без этого у нас есть сильная матрица, но ещё нет формального права сказать:

\[
\boxed{

}
\]

- **Step 33** — подключение к exhaustion/family layer.

```text

```

Это лучше, чем просто “неотрицательность”: у нас есть **coercive-style lower bound** относительно \(R\).

**Coercive-style lower bound** — нижняя оценка не просто \(0\le W\), а вида \(W\ge \theta R\), то есть форма держит положительный запас относительно базовой энергии.

```text
Step 25–26:

  FinitePenaltyCert + matrix identification
  → finite analytic Weil positivity

Step 30:
  boundary-null exhaustion

Next:
  instantiate matrix identification for actual B-spline packet model
```

То есть мы больше не висим в воздухе с CSV. У нас есть theorem-facing путь:

\[
\boxed{
\text{сертифицированная матрица}
\Rightarrow
\text{аналитическая конечная позитивность}.
}
\]

}
\]

# Step 32 — B-spline matrix identification instance

Теперь следующий настоящий шаг:

\[
\boxed{
\textbf{Step 32: instantiate FiniteWeilMatrixModel for B-spline packet space.}
}
\]

Нужно доказать три группы формул.

---

## 1. Boundary rows

Для basis:

\[
\psi_j(u)=\ell^{-1/2}\eta_k\left(\frac{u-u_j}{\ell}\right)
\]

transform:

\[
H_j(z)=\sqrt{\ell}\,e^{zu_j}E_{\ell,k}(z).
\]

Значит:

\[
H_j(1/2)
=
\sqrt{\ell}e^{u_j/2}E_{\ell,k}(1/2),
\]

\[
H_j(-1/2)
=
\sqrt{\ell}e^{-u_j/2}E_{\ell,k}(-1/2).
\]

И так как общий ненулевой множитель можно отбросить в boundary-null condition:

\[
Q_+j=e^{u_j/2},
\qquad
Q_-j=e^{-u_j/2}.
\]

Нужно показать:

\[
\boxed{
H_v(1/2)=0
\iff
\sum_j v_j Q_{+,j}=0
}
\]

и аналогично для \(-1/2\), с учётом общего множителя.

**Boundary row** — строка матрицы \(Q\), которая вычисляет boundary functional по координатам.

---

## 2. Arch matrix

Нужно доказать:

\[
\mathcal A(h_v)
=
v^TAv
\]

где:

\[
A_{ij}
=
\frac{\ell}{2\pi}
\int_{\mathbb R}
\Omega(t)
|E_{\ell,k}(it)|^2
e^{it(u_j-u_i)}dt.
\]

Это идёт из:

\[
H_v(it)=\sum_j v_jH_j(it),
\]

\[
\mathcal A(h_v)
=
\frac1{2\pi}
\int \Omega(t)|H_v(it)|^2dt.
\]

Раскрытие квадрата даёт matrix entries.

**Arch matrix** — матрица Gamma/Archimedean части Weil form.

---

## 3. Prime matrix

Нужно доказать:

\[
\mathcal P(h_v)
=
v^TPv
\]

где:

\[
P_{ij}
=
\sum_{r\log p\le2L}
\frac{\log p}{p^{r/2}}
\left[
r_k\left(\frac{u_j-u_i-r\log p}{\ell}\right)
+
r_k\left(\frac{u_j-u_i+r\log p}{\ell}\right)
\right].
\]

Это идёт из correlation identity:

\[
C_{ij}(a)=
\langle \psi_j,S_a\psi_i\rangle
=
r_k\left(\frac{u_j-u_i-a}{\ell}\right).
\]

И compact support даёт finite cutoff:

\[
r\log p>2L
\Rightarrow
C_{ij}(\pm r\log p)=0.
\]

**Prime matrix** — матрица prime-shift части Weil form.

**Correlation identity** — равенство между overlap shifted bumps и \(r_k\).

Да. **Именно так.** То, что мы обсуждали раньше как “оператор Arch минус оператор Prime” и “пространство, где нули могут жить только на одной прямой”, сейчас стало нормальной proof-архитектурой внутри **PSD-pd**.

Карта соответствия такая:

\[
\boxed{
\text{Arch operator} - \text{Prime operator}
}
\]

стал:

\[
\boxed{
C=A-P.
}
\]

Где **`A`** — **Arch matrix**, то есть матрица архимедовой/Gamma-части; **`P`** — **Prime matrix**, то есть матрица prime-side вклада; **`C=A-P`** — finite Weil matrix, которую мы сертифицируем как положительную.

Дальше твоя идея “сделать третий оператор из разности” стала не наивным \(T^\*T\), а более сильным рабочим трюком:

\[
\boxed{
C=(A-\kappa P_0)-(P-\kappa P_0).
}
\]

То есть:

\[
\boxed{
R_\kappa=A-\kappa P_0,
}
\]

\[
\boxed{
D_\theta=C-\theta R_\kappa.
}
\]

**κ-split** — это перенормировка разности \(A-P\), где мы вытаскиваем из prime-side непрерывную главную массу \(P_0\), чтобы получить более устойчивую базовую энергию.  
**Penalty guard** — это проверка:

\[
D_\theta+\tau Q^TQ\succ0,
\qquad
R_\kappa+\tau Q^TQ\succ0.
\]

Она доказывает positivity на **boundary-null** пространстве, не сертифицируя численный basis для \(\ker Q\).

И это уже закрыто на finite block: `A`, `P`, `P0`, `Q` сидят на interval contract, а manifest/ledger превратил это в `FinitePenaltyCert`. В `INSIGHTS.md` текущая PSD-pd линия именно так и описана: corrected cone → packet-Rayleigh → PSD-pd → closure → LF-pd → G6 → RH, а finite certificate engine является внутренним узлом PSD-pd. fileciteturn3file0
