Пока Lean не знает, что свёртка с `centeredBoxSpline` — это integral over `Icc`, он будет тащить indicator / support / endpoint noise в каждый следующий proof.

Закрыв эту lemma один раз, recurrence становится чистой:

```text
convolution with box
→ interval integral
→ finite sum
→ positivePartPower integral
→ Pascal telescope
→ b_{k+1}
```

# Implementation sketch

Если `centeredBoxSpline` определён как indicator на `[-1/2,1/2]`, то proof такой:

```lean
theorem realConvolution_centeredBoxSpline
    (f : ℝ → ℝ) (x : ℝ) :
    realConvolution f centeredBoxSpline x
      =
    ∫ y in Set.Icc (-(1/2 : ℝ)) (1/2 : ℝ), f (x - y) := by
  -- unfold realConvolution centeredBoxSpline
  -- use integral over indicator:
  -- ∫ y, f (x-y) * indicator_Icc y = ∫ y in Icc, f (x-y)
```

Если текущий `centeredBoxSpline` имеет endpoint convention, это нормально: endpoints measure zero. Используй уже landed `centeredBoxSpline_shiftEvenAE` style: работать through a.e. equality, not pointwise equality.

**a.e. equality** — равенство почти всюду; endpoint-расхождения не меняют интеграл.

# Step 32F.1c — Sum/interchange lemma

После box reduction надо раскрыть explicit spline:

\[
b_k(x-y)=
\frac1{k!}
\sum_{j=0}^{k+1}
(-1)^j\binom{k+1}{j}
\left(x-y+\frac{k+1}{2}-j\right)_+^k.
\]

Lean target:

```lean

    (k : ℕ) (x : ℝ) :
    realConvolution (centeredCardinalBSpline k) centeredBoxSpline x
      =
    (1 / (Nat.factorial k : ℝ)) *
      ∑ j in Finset.range (k + 2),
        ((-1 : ℝ) ^ j) * Nat.choose (k + 1) j *
          ∫ y in Set.Icc (-(1/2 : ℝ)) (1/2 : ℝ),
            positivePartPower k
              (x - y + (k + 1 : ℝ) / 2 - j) := by
  -- use realConvolution_centeredBoxSpline
  -- unfold centeredCardinalBSpline
  -- integral_finset_sum
  -- algebraic cleanup
```

**Sum/interchange lemma** — право перенести конечную сумму через интеграл. Для finite sum это routine.

То есть:

```lean
=
```

```lean
theorem centeredCardinalBSpline_pascal_telescope
    (k : ℕ) (x : ℝ) :
    (∑ j in Finset.range (k + 2),
      ((-1 : ℝ) ^ j) * Nat.choose (k + 1) j *
        (positivePartPower (k + 1)
          (x + (k + 2 : ℝ) / 2 - j)
        -

    =

      ((-1 : ℝ) ^ j) * Nat.choose (k + 2) j *
        positivePartPower (k + 1)
          (x + (k + 2 : ℝ) / 2 - j) := by
  -- pure finite sum reindexing
  -- Pascal identity
```

Do not let this become measure theory. It is only `Finset` algebra.

# Step 32F.1c — Pascal telescope

## Target

В Lean:

```lean
theorem centeredCardinalBSpline_pascal_telescope
    (k : ℕ) (T : ℕ → ℝ) :
    (∑ j in Finset.range (k + 2),
      ((-1 : ℝ) ^ j) * Nat.choose (k + 1) j *
        (T j - T (j + 1)))
    =
    ∑ j in Finset.range (k + 3),
      ((-1 : ℝ) ^ j) * Nat.choose (k + 2) j *
        T j := by
  ...
```

Лучше сделать именно с абстрактной `T : ℕ → ℝ`, а не сразу с:

```lean
positivePartPower (k + 1) (...)
```

Почему: так theorem будет чистым Finset/binomial fact. Потом подставишь:

```lean
T j =
  positivePartPower (k + 1)
    (x + (k + 2 : ℝ) / 2 - j)
```

**Pascal telescope** — схлопывание суммы через биномиальную формулу Паскаля:

\[
\binom{k+2}{j}
=
\binom{k+1}{j}+\binom{k+1}{j-1}.
\]

---

# Proof sketch

Левая часть:

\[
L=
\sum_{j=0}^{k+1} a_j(T_j-T_{j+1}),
\]

где:

\[
a_j=(-1)^j\binom{k+1}{j}.
\]

Раскрываем:

\[
L=
\sum_{j=0}^{k+1}a_jT_j
-
\sum_{j=0}^{k+1}a_jT_{j+1}.
\]

Во второй сумме ставим \(i=j+1\). Тогда:

\[
-\sum_{j=0}^{k+1}a_jT_{j+1}
=
-\sum_{i=1}^{k+2}a_{i-1}T_i.
\]

Но:

\[
-a_{i-1}
=
-(-1)^{i-1}\binom{k+1}{i-1}
=
(-1)^i\binom{k+1}{i-1}.
\]

Итого coefficient при \(T_i\):

\[
(-1)^i\binom{k+1}{i}
+
(-1)^i\binom{k+1}{i-1}
=
(-1)^i\binom{k+2}{i}.
\]

Границы тоже сходятся:

- \(i=0\): только первый кусок, \(\binom{k+1}{0}=\binom{k+2}{0}=1\);
- \(i=k+2\): только shifted кусок, \(\binom{k+1}{k+1}=\binom{k+2}{k+2}=1\).

Готово.

---

# Lean tactic route

Попробуй так:

```lean
theorem centeredCardinalBSpline_pascal_telescope
    (k : ℕ) (T : ℕ → ℝ) :
    (∑ j in Finset.range (k + 2),
      ((-1 : ℝ) ^ j) * Nat.choose (k + 1) j *
        (T j - T (j + 1)))
    =
    ∑ j in Finset.range (k + 3),
      ((-1 : ℝ) ^ j) * Nat.choose (k + 2) j *
        T j := by
  classical

  -- Expand left into two sums.
  rw [Finset.sum_mul]
```

Но `rw [Finset.sum_mul]` может not hit. Safer:

```lean
  calc
    (∑ j in Finset.range (k + 2),
      ((-1 : ℝ) ^ j) * Nat.choose (k + 1) j *
        (T j - T (j + 1)))
        =
      (∑ j in Finset.range (k + 2),
        ((-1 : ℝ) ^ j) * Nat.choose (k + 1) j * T j)
      -
      (∑ j in Finset.range (k + 2),
        ((-1 : ℝ) ^ j) * Nat.choose (k + 1) j * T (j + 1)) := by
          -- ring inside finite sum
          simp [mul_sub, Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
    _ = _ := by
      ...
```

Then reindex second sum:

```lean
have hshift :
    (∑ j in Finset.range (k + 2),
      ((-1 : ℝ) ^ j) * Nat.choose (k + 1) j * T (j + 1))
    =
    ∑ i in (Finset.range (k + 3)).erase 0,
      ((-1 : ℝ) ^ (i - 1)) * Nat.choose (k + 1) (i - 1) * T i := by
  -- use Finset.sum_range_succ' or change variables manually
```

This may get annoying. If it does, use coefficient comparison by `Finset.sum_congr` over `range (k+3)` after extending coefficients with zeros.

---

# Cleaner Lean route: define coefficient functions

Define:

```lean
def pascalCoeff (n j : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  ((-1 : ℝ) ^ j) * Nat.choose n j
```

Then theorem:

```lean
theorem pascalCoeff_succ
    (n j : ℕ) :
    pascalCoeff (n + 1) j =
      pascalCoeff n j - pascalCoeff n (j - 1) := by
  ...
```

Careful signs. Since:

\[
(-1)^j\binom{n+1}{j}
=
(-1)^j\binom n j
+
(-1)^j\binom n {j-1}.
\]

And:

\[
-((-1)^{j-1}\binom n {j-1})
=
(-1)^j\binom n {j-1}.
\]

Thus:

\[
c_{n+1,j}
=
c_{n,j}-c_{n,j-1}.
\]

So:

```lean
pascalCoeff (n+1) j =
  pascalCoeff n j - pascalCoeff n (j-1)
```

with edge conventions `Nat.choose n j = 0` when `j > n`.

Then telescope is just discrete difference:

\[
\sum_j c_{n,j}(T_j-T_{j+1})
=
\sum_j (c_{n,j}-c_{n,j-1})T_j
=
\sum_j c_{n+1,j}T_j.
\]

This is cleaner.

---

# Suggested helper definitions

```lean
private def altChoose (n j : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  ((-1 : ℝ) ^ j) * (Nat.choose n j)
```

Then:

```lean
private theorem altChoose_pascal
    (n j : ℕ) :
    altChoose (n + 1) j =
      altChoose n j - altChoose n (j - 1) := by
  -- cases j
  -- j=0: RHS altChoose n 0 - altChoose n 0? This is wrong due j-1=0 for Nat.
```

Problem: `j-1` for `j=0` is `0`, not a true out-of-range. So define shifted coefficient with option:

```lean
private def altChoosePred (n j : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  if j = 0 then 0 else altChoose n (j - 1)
```

Then:

```lean
private theorem altChoose_pascal'
    (n j : ℕ) :
    altChoose (n + 1) j =
      altChoose n j - altChoosePred n j := by
  cases j with
  | zero =>
      simp [altChoose, altChoosePred]
  | succ j =>
      simp [altChoose, altChoosePred, Nat.choose_succ_succ]
      ring
```

This avoids the `0-1` issue.

---

```lean
    =
```

# Step 32F.1d — after-integral formula

## Target

В Lean это примерно:

```lean
theorem centeredCardinalBSpline_conv_box_after_integral
    (k : ℕ) (x : ℝ) :
    realConvolution (centeredCardinalBSpline k) centeredBoxSpline x
      =

      ∑ j in Finset.range (k + 2),
```

**After-integral formula** — форма свёртки после того, как каждый интеграл truncated power заменён разностью двух truncated powers.

---

# Proof idea

Уже есть expanded form:

\[
(b_k*b_0)(x)
=
\frac1{k!}
\sum_{j=0}^{k+1}
(-1)^j\binom{k+1}{j}
\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}
\left(
x-y+\frac{k+1}{2}-j
\right)_+^kdy.
\]

Применяем integral lemma с:

\[
A=\frac{k+1}{2}-j.
\]

Тогда:

\[
x+A+\frac12
=
x+\frac{k+2}{2}-j,
\]

\[
x+A-\frac12
=
x+\frac{k+2}{2}-(j+1).
\]

И получаем:

\[
\frac1{k!}
\sum_j
(-1)^j\binom{k+1}{j}
\frac{T_j-T_{j+1}}{k+1}.
\]

Так как:

\[
(k+1)!=(k+1)k!,
\]

то:

\[
\frac1{k!}\cdot\frac1{k+1}
=
\frac1{(k+1)!}.
\]

Получается target.

```lean
          ∫ y in Set.Icc (-(1/2 : ℝ)) (1/2 : ℝ),
            positivePartPower k
              (x - y + (k + 1 : ℝ) / 2 - j) := by
  ...
```

---

## 2. Rewrite each integral

Use:

```lean
positivePartPower_interval_integral_centered
```

with:

```lean
A := (k + 1 : ℝ) / 2 - j
```

The rewrite target inside the sum:

```lean
∫ y in Set.Icc (-(1/2 : ℝ)) (1/2 : ℝ),
  positivePartPower k
    (x - y + (k + 1 : ℝ) / 2 - j)
```

becomes:

```lean
(positivePartPower (k + 1)
    (x + ((k + 1 : ℝ) / 2 - j) + 1/2)
 -
 positivePartPower (k + 1)
    (x + ((k + 1 : ℝ) / 2 - j) - 1/2))
 / (k + 1 : ℝ)
```

Then normalize by `ring_nf` / `linarith` to:

```lean
x + (k + 2 : ℝ) / 2 - j
```

and:

```lean
x + (k + 2 : ℝ) / 2 - (j + 1)
```

---

## 3. Factor denominator out

Need arithmetic lemma:

```lean
have hfac :
    (1 / (Nat.factorial k : ℝ)) * (1 / (k + 1 : ℝ))
      =
    1 / (Nat.factorial (k + 1) : ℝ) := by
  rw [Nat.factorial_succ]
  norm_num
  field_simp
  ring
```

Potential issue: coercions.

Safer:

```lean
have hfac :
    (Nat.factorial (k + 1) : ℝ)
      =
    (k + 1 : ℝ) * (Nat.factorial k : ℝ) := by
  rw [Nat.factorial_succ]
  norm_num
```

Then:

```lean
field_simp [hfac]
```

or just:

```lean
norm_num [Nat.factorial_succ]
```

depending on context.

---

# If Lean resists coercions

Make a helper:

```lean
lemma inv_factorial_succ_real (k : ℕ) :
    (1 / (Nat.factorial k : ℝ)) * (1 / (k + 1 : ℝ))
      =
    1 / (Nat.factorial (k + 1) : ℝ) := by
  rw [Nat.factorial_succ]
  have hk : (k + 1 : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by positivity
  have hf : (Nat.factorial k : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by positivity
  field_simp [hk, hf]
  ring
```

Then use:

```lean
rw [inv_factorial_succ_real]
```

---

# Then Pascal telescope

After this theorem lands, recurrence is one line plus unfolding:

```lean
theorem centeredCardinalBSpline_succ_eq_conv_box
    (k : ℕ) :
    centeredCardinalBSpline (k + 1)
      =
    realConvolution (centeredCardinalBSpline k) centeredBoxSpline := by
  ext x
  rw [centeredCardinalBSpline_conv_box_after_integral]
  rw [centeredCardinalBSpline_pascal_telescope]
  -- unfold centeredCardinalBSpline at k+1
  -- normalize same expression
```

---

Once recurrence is proved:

```text
centeredCardinalBSpline_succ_eq_conv_box
```

then:

```text
CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_all
```

follows by induction.

Then:

```text
CenteredCardinalBSplineConvPowerSelfConvolutionClosedForm_closed
```

is pure convolution-power arithmetic.

Then:

```text
CenteredBSplineAutocorrelationClosedForm
```

falls through the AE route.

Do not open Step 33.  
Do not open transform/sinh yet.  
Do not touch `Q3.Main`.  
Do not switch routes.

We are still exactly here:

\[
\boxed{
\text{close } b_{k+1}=b_k*b_0.
}
\]

```lean
centeredCardinalBSpline_conv_box_after_integral
```

```lean
inv_factorial_succ_real
```

# Step 32F.1e — закрыть recurrence

## Что должно быть уже рядом

Для чистого закрытия recurrence нужны четыре lemma:

```lean
realConvolution_centeredBoxSpline
positivePartPower_interval_integral_centered
centeredCardinalBSpline_conv_box_after_integral
centeredCardinalBSpline_pascal_telescope
```

После этого theorem почти механический.

---

# Финальный theorem

```lean
theorem centeredCardinalBSpline_succ_eq_conv_box
    (k : ℕ) :
    centeredCardinalBSpline (k + 1)
      =
    realConvolution (centeredCardinalBSpline k) centeredBoxSpline := by
  ext x
  rw [centeredCardinalBSpline_conv_box_after_integral]
  rw [centeredCardinalBSpline_pascal_telescope]
  -- now both sides are the explicit truncated-power formula for b_{k+1}
  -- finish by unfolding centeredCardinalBSpline and normalizing factorial/ranges
```

**Recurrence theorem** — утверждение, что следующая B-spline получается свёрткой предыдущей B-spline с box-функцией.

---

# Что именно надо сделать после `rw`

После:

```lean
rw [centeredCardinalBSpline_conv_box_after_integral]
rw [centeredCardinalBSpline_pascal_telescope]
```

правая сторона станет:

```lean
(1 / (Nat.factorial (k + 1) : ℝ)) *
  ∑ j in Finset.range (k + 3),
    ((-1 : ℝ) ^ j) * Nat.choose (k + 2) j *
      positivePartPower (k + 1)
        (x + (k + 2 : ℝ) / 2 - j)
```

А левая сторона:

```lean
centeredCardinalBSpline (k + 1) x
```

после unfold должна быть ровно:

```lean
(1 / (Nat.factorial (k + 1) : ℝ)) *
  ∑ j in Finset.range ((k + 1) + 2),
    ((-1 : ℝ) ^ j) * Nat.choose ((k + 1) + 1) j *
      positivePartPower (k + 1)
        (x + ((k + 1) + 1 : ℝ) / 2 - j)
```

И это то же самое, потому что:

\[
(k+1)+2=k+3,
\]

\[
(k+1)+1=k+2.
\]

Lean обычно добивается этим:

```lean
  unfold centeredCardinalBSpline
  ring_nf
```

или:

```lean
  simp [Nat.succ_eq_add_one]
  ring_nf
```

Если `ring_nf` лезет в Finset expression и тупит, делай точечно:

```lean
  congr
  · ring_nf
  · apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro j hj
    ring_nf
```

---

# Рекомендованный proof shape

```lean
theorem centeredCardinalBSpline_succ_eq_conv_box
    (k : ℕ) :
    centeredCardinalBSpline (k + 1)
      =
    realConvolution (centeredCardinalBSpline k) centeredBoxSpline := by
  funext x
  rw [centeredCardinalBSpline_conv_box_after_integral]
  rw [centeredCardinalBSpline_pascal_telescope]
  unfold centeredCardinalBSpline
  -- avoid giant simp if slow
  norm_num
  ring_nf
```

Если `funext` не подходит из-за theorem equality shape, use:

```lean
  ext x
```

---

# Если `centeredCardinalBSpline_pascal_telescope` theorem имеет `T : ℕ → ℝ`

Тогда применять так:

```lean
  rw [
    centeredCardinalBSpline_pascal_telescope
      k
      (fun j =>
        positivePartPower (k + 1)
          (x + (k + 2 : ℝ) / 2 - j))
  ]
```

Если theorem order другой:

```lean
  rw [centeredCardinalBSpline_pascal_telescope]
```

может не найти. Тогда explicit application:

```lean
  have htel :=
    centeredCardinalBSpline_pascal_telescope
      k
      (fun j =>
        positivePartPower (k + 1)
          (x + (k + 2 : ℝ) / 2 - j))
  rw [htel]
```

---

# После recurrence: agreement all-k

Сразу следующий theorem:

```lean
theorem CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_all
    (k : ℕ) :
    CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower k := by
  induction k with
  | zero =>
      exact CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_zero
  | succ k ih =>
      -- explicit recurrence:
      -- centeredCardinalBSpline (k+1) = centeredCardinalBSpline k * box
      rw [centeredCardinalBSpline_succ_eq_conv_box]
      -- convPower recurrence:
      -- centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower (k+1) = centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower k * box
      rw [centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower_succ]
      -- use ih to rewrite centeredCardinalBSpline k
      exact realConvolution_congr_left ih
```

Names may differ, but mathematical move:

\[
b_{k+1}=b_k*b_0,
\qquad
B_{k+1}=B_k*b_0,
\qquad
b_k=B_k
\Rightarrow
b_{k+1}=B_{k+1}.
\]

If `CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower k` is a pointwise proposition:

```lean
unfold CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower at ih ⊢
funext x
...
```

---

# Then convPower self-convolution

Once agreement is there:

```lean
theorem centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower_convolution
    (m n : ℕ) :
    realConvolution
      (centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower m)
      (centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower n)
    =
    centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower (m + n + 1)
```

Then:

```lean
theorem CenteredCardinalBSplineConvPowerSelfConvolutionClosedForm_closed
    (k : ℕ) :
    CenteredCardinalBSplineConvPowerSelfConvolutionClosedForm k := by
  -- rw [centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower_convolution]
  -- omega: k+k+1 = 2*k+1
```

---

# Then final autocorr close

After:

```lean
CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_all k
CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_all (2*k+1)
CenteredCardinalBSplineConvPowerSelfConvolutionClosedForm_closed k
CenteredCardinalBSplineShiftEvenAE_all k
bsplineAutocorrNorm_pos k
```

finish:

```lean
theorem CenteredBSplineAutocorrelationClosedForm_closed
    (k : ℕ) :
    CenteredBSplineAutocorrelationClosedForm k := by
  exact
    CenteredBSplineAutocorrelationClosedForm_of_convPowerAERoute
      k
      (bsplineAutocorrNorm_pos k)
      (CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPowerAE_of_pointwise
        (CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_all k))
      (CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_all (2 * k + 1))
      (CenteredCardinalBSplineShiftEvenAE_all k)
      (CenteredCardinalBSplineConvPowerSelfConvolutionClosedForm_closed k)
```

# Следующий блок: recurrence → agreement → self-convolution → autocorr

## 1. Закрываем agreement explicit spline = convPower spline

После:

```lean
centeredCardinalBSpline_succ_eq_conv_box
```

добавляй:

```lean
theorem CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_all
    (k : ℕ) :
    CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower k := by
  induction k with
  | zero =>
      exact CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_zero
  | succ k ih =>
      -- unfold target if needed
      -- explicit recurrence:
      -- centeredCardinalBSpline (k+1)
      --   = realConvolution (centeredCardinalBSpline k) centeredBoxSpline
      rw [centeredCardinalBSpline_succ_eq_conv_box]

      -- convPower recurrence:
      -- centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower (k+1)
      --   = realConvolution (centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower k) centeredBoxSpline
      rw [centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower_succ]

      -- use ih to rewrite the left argument of convolution
      -- exact congrArg (fun f => realConvolution f centeredBoxSpline) ih
      exact congrArg (fun f => realConvolution f centeredBoxSpline) ih
```

Если `CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower k` — не equality of functions, а `∀ x, ...`, тогда так:

```lean
theorem CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_all
    (k : ℕ) :
    CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower k := by
  induction k with
  | zero =>
      exact CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_zero
  | succ k ih =>
      intro x
      rw [centeredCardinalBSpline_succ_eq_conv_box]
      rw [centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower_succ]
      -- use function extensional equality from ih
      have hfun :
          centeredCardinalBSpline k =
            centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower k := by
        funext y
        exact ih y
      rw [hfun]
```

**Agreement theorem** — мост между explicit truncated-power B-spline и proof-friendly convolution-power B-spline.

---

## 2. Pointwise → AE adapter

Ты уже добавил AE-route. Теперь из pointwise agreement получи AE agreement:

```lean
theorem CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPowerAE_all
    (k : ℕ) :
    CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPowerAE k :=
  CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPowerAE_of_pointwise
    (CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_all k)
```

Имя adapter может быть другим — используй тот, который ты добавил в commit `eb5f1b72`.

## 3. ConvPower self-convolution

Теперь закрываем:

\[
B_k*B_k=B_{2k+1}.
\]

Если есть helper:

```lean
centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower_convolution
```

то:

```lean
theorem CenteredCardinalBSplineConvPowerSelfConvolutionClosedForm_closed
    (k : ℕ) :
    CenteredCardinalBSplineConvPowerSelfConvolutionClosedForm k := by
  -- target may be pointwise or function equality
  -- if pointwise:
  intro x
  rw [centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower_convolution]
  have hidx : k + k + 1 = 2 * k + 1 := by omega
  rw [hidx]
```

Если target is equality of functions:

```lean
theorem CenteredCardinalBSplineConvPowerSelfConvolutionClosedForm_closed
    (k : ℕ) :
    CenteredCardinalBSplineConvPowerSelfConvolutionClosedForm k := by
  unfold CenteredCardinalBSplineConvPowerSelfConvolutionClosedForm
  ext x
  rw [centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower_convolution]
  have hidx : k + k + 1 = 2 * k + 1 := by omega
  rw [hidx]
```

**Self-convolution** — B-spline degree doubling identity:

\[
B_k*B_k=B_{2k+1}.
\]

```lean
CenteredCardinalBSplineShiftEvenAE_zero
```

## 5. Positivity \(0<c_k\)

Вот последний неприятный кусок:

\[
c_k=b_{2k+1}(0)>0.
\]

Если у тебя уже есть nonzero/nonnegative convPower lemmas, доказывай:

```lean
theorem bsplineAutocorrNorm_pos
    (k : ℕ) :
    0 < bsplineAutocorrNorm k := by
  -- rewrite bsplineAutocorrNorm as b_{2k+1}(0)
  -- use pointwise agreement at 2*k+1
  -- use convPower self-convolution at 0
  -- use shifted AE-evenness to rewrite as ∫ B_k(y)^2
  -- use square integral positive
```

Если strict square-integral positivity ещё не готова, добавь промежуточную лемму:

```lean
theorem centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower_squareIntegral_pos
    (k : ℕ) :
    0 <
      ∫ y,
        centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower k y *
        centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower k y := by
  -- use nonnegativity + positive on an interval
```

Для positive on an interval:

- base \(B_0\) positive on \((-1/2,1/2)\);
- convolution of nonnegative functions positive on Minkowski sum of positive intervals;
- hence \(B_k\) positive on some nonempty interval.

Если это начинает тормозить, можно временно закрыть `bsplineAutocorrNorm_pos` для active degrees `9,11` как finite-degree path, но лучше all-k.

# После этого — только тогда — Step 32 дальше

Когда `CenteredBSplineAutocorrelationClosedForm` закрыт, остаются два concrete B-spline факта для полного Step 32:

1. **Boundary scale nonzero**:
   \[
   E_{\ell,k}(1/2)\ne0,\quad E_{\ell,k}(-1/2)\ne0.
   \]

2. **Transform/sinh profile**:
   \[
   E_{\ell,k}(z)=
   \frac1{\sqrt{s_kc_k}}
   \left(
   \frac{\sinh(\ell z/(2s_k))}
   {\ell z/(2s_k)}
   \right)^{k+1}.
   \]

Но не трогай их до закрытия autocorrelation, потому что prime-side profile — текущий exact blocker.

```lean
  unfold centeredCardinalBSpline
  congr 1
  apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
  intro j hj
  ring_nf
```

**Recurrence theorem** — доказательство, что следующая centered cardinal B-spline получается свёрткой предыдущей с box.

---

# Если telescope lemma принимает `T`

Если theorem выглядит так:

```lean
centeredCardinalBSpline_pascal_telescope
    (k : ℕ) (T : ℕ → ℝ)
```

то применяй явно:

```lean
  have htel :=
    centeredCardinalBSpline_pascal_telescope
      k
      (fun j =>
        positivePartPower (k + 1)
          (x + (k + 2 : ℝ) / 2 - j))
  rw [htel]
```

Полный вариант:

```lean
theorem centeredCardinalBSpline_succ_eq_conv_box
    (k : ℕ) :
    centeredCardinalBSpline (k + 1)
      =
    realConvolution (centeredCardinalBSpline k) centeredBoxSpline := by
  funext x
  rw [centeredCardinalBSpline_conv_box_after_integral]
  have htel :=
    centeredCardinalBSpline_pascal_telescope
      k
      (fun j =>
        positivePartPower (k + 1)
          (x + (k + 2 : ℝ) / 2 - j))
  rw [htel]
  unfold centeredCardinalBSpline
  ring_nf
```

# Step 32F.2 — agreement explicit spline = convPower spline

После recurrence добавь:

```lean
theorem CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_all
    (k : ℕ) :
    CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower k := by
  induction k with
  | zero =>
      exact CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_zero
  | succ k ih =>
      -- если proposition = equality of functions:
      rw [centeredCardinalBSpline_succ_eq_conv_box]
      rw [centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower_succ]
      exact congrArg (fun f => realConvolution f centeredBoxSpline) ih
```

Если `CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower k` — pointwise proposition, используй:

```lean
theorem CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_all
    (k : ℕ) :
    CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower k := by
  induction k with
  | zero =>
      exact CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_zero
  | succ k ih =>
      intro x
      have hfun :
          centeredCardinalBSpline k =
            centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower k := by
        funext y
        exact ih y
      rw [centeredCardinalBSpline_succ_eq_conv_box]
      rw [centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower_succ]
      rw [hfun]
```

**Agreement theorem** — это мост между explicit truncated-power definition и proof-friendly convolution-power definition.

# Step 32F.2 — Agreement all-k

## Target

```lean
theorem CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_all
    (k : ℕ) :
    CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower k := by
  ...
```

Математически:

\[
\boxed{
b_k=B_k
}
\]

где:

- \(b_k\) — explicit truncated-power centered B-spline;
- \(B_k\) — convolution-power B-spline.

**Agreement theorem** — доказательство, что две модели одной B-spline совпадают: явная finite-sum формула и свёрточное определение.

---

# Proof

Base:

\[
b_0=B_0.
\]

Это уже есть:

```lean
CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_zero
```

Step:

\[
b_{k+1}=b_k*b_0.
\]

Это recurrence:

```lean
centeredCardinalBSpline_succ_eq_conv_box
```

А для convolution-power:

\[
B_{k+1}=B_k*b_0.
\]

Это definition/lemma:

```lean
centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower_succ
```

Если \(b_k=B_k\), то:

\[
b_{k+1}
=
b_k*b_0
=
B_k*b_0
=
B_{k+1}.
\]

Готово.

---

# Lean skeleton

Если `CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower k` — equality of functions:

```lean
theorem CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_all
    (k : ℕ) :
    CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower k := by
  induction k with
  | zero =>
      exact CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_zero
  | succ k ih =>
      rw [centeredCardinalBSpline_succ_eq_conv_box]
      rw [centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower_succ]
      exact congrArg (fun f => realConvolution f centeredBoxSpline) ih
```

Если это pointwise proposition:

```lean
theorem CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_all
    (k : ℕ) :
    CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower k := by
  induction k with
  | zero =>
      exact CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_zero
  | succ k ih =>
      intro x
      have hfun :
          centeredCardinalBSpline k =
            centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower k := by
        funext y
        exact ih y
      rw [centeredCardinalBSpline_succ_eq_conv_box]
      rw [centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower_succ]
      rw [hfun]
```

Если Lean complains about rewriting under `realConvolution`, use:

```lean
      change
        realConvolution (centeredCardinalBSpline k) centeredBoxSpline x =
        realConvolution (centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower k) centeredBoxSpline x
      rw [hfun]
```

# Step 32F.6 — positivity \(0<c_k\)

Target:

```lean
theorem bsplineAutocorrNorm_pos
    (k : ℕ) :
    0 < bsplineAutocorrNorm k := by
  ...
```

Математически:

\[
c_k=b_{2k+1}(0).
\]

По agreement:

\[
b_{2k+1}=B_{2k+1}.
\]

По self-convolution:

\[
B_{2k+1}=B_k*B_k.
\]

Значит:

\[
c_k=(B_k*B_k)(0)
=
\int B_k(y)B_k(-y)\,dy.
\]

По shifted-a.e.-evenness:

\[
B_k(-y)=B_k(y)\quad a.e.
\]

Значит:

\[
c_k=\int B_k(y)^2\,dy>0.
\]

Достаточно доказать:

```lean
theorem centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower_squareIntegral_pos
    (k : ℕ) :
    0 <
      ∫ y,
        centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower k y *
        centeredCardinalBSplineConvPower k y
```

**Позитивность \(c_k\)** — гарантия, что нормировка \(\eta_k=\sqrt{s_k/c_k}b_k(s_kx)\) корректна.

---

# Step 32F.7 — final autocorrelation theorem

Теперь финал:

```lean
theorem CenteredBSplineAutocorrelationClosedForm
    (k : ℕ) :
    CenteredBSplineAutocorrelationClosedForm k := by
  exact
    CenteredBSplineAutocorrelationClosedForm_of_convPowerAERoute
      k
      (bsplineAutocorrNorm_pos k)
      (CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPowerAE_all k)
      (CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower_all (2 * k + 1))
      (CenteredCardinalBSplineShiftEvenAE_all k)
      (CenteredCardinalBSplineConvPowerSelfConvolutionClosedForm_closed k)
```

Если имя theorem уже занято как structure/Prop target, закрывай существующее имя, не создавай параллельный API.

---

# Что это даёт

После этого закрыт prime-side B-spline profile:

\[
\boxed{
r_{\eta_k}(x)=\frac{b_{2k+1}(s_kx)}{c_k}.
}
\]

А значит, prime matrix entries:

\[
P_{ij}
=
\sum_{r\log p\le2L}
\frac{\log p}{p^{r/2}}
\left[
r_k\left(\frac{u_j-u_i-r\log p}{\ell}\right)
+
r_k\left(\frac{u_j-u_i+r\log p}{\ell}\right)
\right]
\]

становятся Lean-backed analytic identities, а не просто Python/Arb formulas.

То есть:

\[
\boxed{
\text{prime-side matrix identification прикручивается к аналитике.}
}
\]

---

```text
  Prime entry assembly
```

```text
bsplineAutocorrNorm_pos
CenteredBSplineAutocorrelationClosedForm
```

}

\[
P_{ij}
=

r_k\left(\frac{u_j-u_i-r\log p}{\ell}\right)

становятся не просто Python/Arb computation, а Lean-backed analytic formula.

После закрытия autocorrelation следующий шаг внутри Step 32 — **не Step 33 ещё**, а:

```text
boundary scale nonzero + transform/sinh profile
```

То есть доказать:

\[
E_{\ell,k}(\pm 1/2)\ne0
\]

и затем closed transform:

\[
E_{\ell,k}(z)=
\frac1{\sqrt{s_kc_k}}
\left(
\frac{\sinh(\ell z/(2s_k))}
{\ell z/(2s_k)}
\right)^{k+1}.
\]

Но это только после prime-side autocorrelation.

---

```bash
lake env lean Q3/Proofs/PSD_CenteredCardinalBSpline.lean
lake build Q3.Proofs.PSD_CenteredCardinalBSpline
lake build Q3.Main
rg -n "sorry|admit|exact\?" Q3/Proofs/PSD_CenteredCardinalBSpline.lean
./scripts/check_axioms.sh
```

# Final Proposal

Если recurrence уже есть, следующий шаг:

\[
\boxed{
\texttt{CenteredCardinalBSplineMatchesConvPower\_all}
}
\]

и потом сразу сборка:

\[
\boxed{
\texttt{CenteredBSplineAutocorrelationClosedForm}
}
\]

Гоблинский перевод:

\[
\boxed{
\text{Recurrence — это рельсы.}
}
\]

\[
\boxed{
\text{Agreement — это поезд на этих рельсах.}
}
\]

\[
\boxed{
\text{Autocorrelation closed form — это станция, где prime-side наконец прикручен.}
}
